### S4.1a delivery — dormant family contract and publisher projector

S4.1a新增source-relative Narrative family contract：`surface-owner.narrative`拥有blocking Dialogue single root，
History保持同owner的exact-parent transient child而不进入stable target vector。Family construction以descriptor-safe dense
own-data snapshot验证exact aggregate definition sidecars与normalized slot recipe，并证明admission authority、publisher
registry与composite kernel来自同一composition配置；该seam不进入UI root、`./internal`或package export barrel。

Composition-owned bridge领取一个fresh publisher lease并注册exact unpublished baseline。Registration前或registration
throw会raw-cleanup已领取但未注册的lease；registration同步notification后还会重验exact current lease与unpublished
baseline，失败则经composition disposal authority退休旧lease。Listener若已retire predecessor并安装合法successor，失败
清理只作用于旧lease，不能删除successor。Initial null不签发source或occurrence；`say`、`choice`、`pause`、
`presentation_barrier`与`custom`的每个新semantic occurrence都发布dedicated source revision与fresh Surface occurrence，
nonempty→empty只推进source，empty后reopen仍fresh。同一semantic occurrence先比较full normalized
`PendingInteractionV1` canonical bytes：byte-equal
复用exact accepted identity，任一字段漂移closed fault；bridge只保留WeakMap-bound admitted frame proof，不建立第二份
semantic writable state。

Candidate preflight callable在construction时以exact receiver捕获一次；每个new/retry candidate在任何source/occurrence或
runtime identity allocation前执行。它只接受descriptor-captured exact tagged结果：`captured`、
`rejected / narrative.renderer_missing`、`rejected / narrative.required_port_missing`（closed bounded `portId`）或
`faulted / narrative.candidate_preflight_faulted`。Malformed/throwing result或snapshot稳定归family fault；required ports必须为
opaque identity，voice replay与quick-menu contribution只接受`null`或opaque identity。无论callback返回、throw还是经
Proxy捕获失败，bridge都先重验exact lease、accepted baseline与reservation generation；同步nested reconcile/dispose/
terminal transition first-wins，loser零issuance。Readiness-failed greater-same retry重新preflight，保留target occurrence并只在
成功后推进source与分配fresh runtime candidate；rejection保留exact failure gap、frame、cursor、topology与notification。

本批尚未实现physical stable-action admission、automatic controller-attempt lease、semantic dispatch双fence、React Host、
History transient lifecycle或live Story/application ingress；这些分别由S4.1b、S4.2与S4.3拥有。没有新增public API、
generic stable result code、barrel export或live feature声明。批准的Narrative专用preflight result仅为source-relative
package-internal contract，关闭了本批发现的generic result-code stop而不扩大generic Surface receipt。

验证：focused `5 files / 138 tests`、UI package `79 files / 989 tests`、全量
`253 files / 3917 tests`与完整`deno task check` green。S4.1a没有接browser/build/live application路径，因此未机械重跑
browser、examples或prebuilt；最近已有的Engine browser `101 / 101`、examples `45 passed / 2 skipped`与prebuilt Player
`38 / 38`只作为既有证据，不表述为S4.1a HEAD上的重复执行。S4.1a完成时current/next均为S4.1b dormant
stable-action/controller-attempt admission；该checkpoint现由下述S4.1b.0 delivery取代。

### S4.1b.0 delivery — shared contract-bound action gate and stable topology proof

S4.1b.0把既有Managed Surface action binding抽为family-neutral、contract-bound route adapter。Live default
`createManagedSurfaceActionBindingV1`仍从exact transient Coordinator publication导出同一binding contract，并以构造期
捕获的exact receiver/callable适配新route authority；既有envelope、input fallthrough/consume、Surface receipt、registration/
revision retain与dispose行为逐项保持。相同contract只在同一authority下复用binding；foreign authority、clone、second claim、
disposed publication与同步reentry均fail closed，continuation throw后route fence会释放，后续合法调用可恢复。

Authentic binding只允许一次package-internal continuation claim。每次claimed route先完成binding publication、physical gesture、
Surface route，再次完成publication/gesture/publication currentness检查，只有exact `unchanged / surface.action_routed`才调用一次
owner continuation；任一stale/rejected/faulted route对continuation为零，direct untagged InputRouter仍按原优先级fall through，
claim后绕过claimed facade直接调用原binding只会consume已认证输入而不会调用continuation或lower handler。Dispose同时seal
claim与registration，repeat dispose不复活route。

同一composition-owned stable composite authority新增只读stable action seam：它从shared topology唯一current input owner捕获
exact binding contract，并只为ready+active、直接对应current desired stable target的实例签发opaque direct-target proof。Stable
route沿用application epoch → topology revision → instance → whole-topology input owner → routing lease → action catalog precedence，且不修改
composite state或通知listener。Source advance、replacement、suspension、empty/dispose/terminal、foreign/clone/revoked proof都会使
旧proof稳定失效。Retain-current replacement期间旧runtime route仍可保持physical Surface receipt，但该capture的
`directTarget/sourceRevision/targetProof`均为`null`，因此不能被后续Narrative semantic admission误认作current desired target。

本批没有定义Narrative action ID到semantic callable/result的closed mapping，没有调用Base semantic dispatch，没有建立automatic
controller-attempt lease，也没有接Narrative bridge、React Host、live Story/application ingress、public/internal barrel或feature
声明。因此S4.1b.0不宣称Narrative semantic admission或live cutover；S4.1b.1必须先裁决并冻结semantic callable/action mapping，
再消费本批的physical route与direct-target proof。

验证：focused `7 files / 172 tests`、UI package `79 files / 999 tests`、全量
`253 files / 3927 tests`、完整`deno task check`、Engine browser `101 / 101`、examples
`45 passed / 2 skipped`与prebuilt Player `38 / 38`均green。S4.1b.0已完成；current/next均为
S4.1b.1；该checkpoint现由下述S4.1b.1a delivery细分并取代。

### S4.1b.1a delivery — authenticated action continuation context

Claimed continuation现接收source-relative、frozen exact `{ actionId, attempt }`。该input只会在exact Surface route、
current input publication与current physical gesture全部通过后构造；`actionId`来自authenticated envelope，`attempt`保持exact
identity且不被读取或复制，因此attempt内伪造的action ID不能替换route gate已经认证的action ID。Unclaimed/default binding、
direct untagged event、stale publication/gesture、Surface reject、dispose、reentry与consumer throw均保留S4.1b.0既有行为。

本corrective没有Narrative action-to-resolution mapping、semantic callable、mapped proof、automatic controller attempt或
non-test claimant，也没有Narrative/React/Web ingress、public/internal barrel、package export或generic receipt变化。Raw semantic
callable不会先于mapping/proof authority暴露；后续切片必须同批冻结closed mapping并只允许same-authority authenticated proof触发
exact captured callable。验证：focused `7 files / 172 tests`、UI package `79 files / 999 tests`、全量
`253 files / 3927 tests`与完整`deno task check` green。本批没有改变browser/build graph，因此未机械重跑browser、examples或
prebuilt；S4.1b.0的`101 / 101`、`45 passed / 2 skipped`与`38 / 38`仅作最近已有证据。S4.1b.1a已完成；
该checkpoint现由下述S4.1b.1b.0 delivery细分并取代。

### S4.1b.1b.0 delivery — choice-only physical semantic vertical

本批完成第一条closed Narrative physical-to-semantic vertical：source-relative Narrative authority只为exact current、
ready+active且pending为choice的stable target签发frozen opaque choice attempt；attempt绑定exact target、source revision、
semantic occurrence、frame、choice ID与captured semantic port，但不暴露raw receiver或callable。Shared managed action route仍先验证
Surface/input publication/gesture，claimed continuation再验证authenticated `narrative.choose`、attempt provenance和exact current
direct-target/frame/semantic occurrence；最后一次current proof通过后，才以exact captured receiver/callable dispatch frozen
`{ expectedOccurrenceId, resolution: { kind: "choose", choiceId } }`。Authentic wrong/unmapped action返回`unmapped`且不读取或
消耗choice attempt；unknown、clone、foreign、stale或repeat attempt不会触发semantic dispatch。Exact semantic callable同步throw
被归一为rejected `completion` Promise，route仍返回`dispatched`，且不会回滚或改写Surface/composite state。

Physical admission由同一Narrative bridge的exact active claim约束。重复construction在创建binding或改写gesture前拒绝；
construction中途失败只清除自己的exact claim。Dispose先seal claimed route/binding，再仅在bridge record仍指向自身claim时清除；
旧或重复dispose不能清除successor claim，同一bridge/InputRouter在dispose并建立fresh ready target后可以创建fresh successor
admission。Shared continuation的first-wins、同步reentry、consumer throw与dispose fence继续沿用S4.1b.0既有合同。

本批只映射`narrative.choose`。其余Narrative action mapping、automatic/controller attempt、Host/History/React mount、live
Story/application ingress、public Story API、public/internal barrel、package export及generic receipt变化均不是本批目标；stable
Narrative ingress仍是source-relative dormant seam，不构成live cutover。验证：focused `7 files / 187 tests`；UI package
`79 files / 1005 tests`、全量`253 files / 3933 tests`、完整check green。本批没有改变browser/build graph，未机械重跑
browser、examples或prebuilt；`101 / 101`、`45 passed / 2 skipped`与`38 / 38`仅作先前已有证据。S4.1b.1b.0已完成；
该checkpoint现由下述S4.1b.1b.1a delivery继续细分并取代。

### S4.1b.1b.1a delivery — skippable-pause physical resume vertical

本批在同一source-relative Narrative physical action authority上完成pause的manual resume vertical。Factory只为current
ready+active choice或pause构造一个exact live admission claim；preparing、retained-only、suspended与其他pending kind保持
unavailable。任意ready pause不论`skippable`都可持有同一managed physical binding，以便后续Host继续承载History与player
controls；`skippable: false`只是不签发manual semantic capability，不建立第二factory、parallel input writer或按kind分叉的
lifecycle。Duplicate construction在改写gesture或binding前拒绝，construction/postcheck failure只清除自身exact claim；dispose
seal route并释放自身claim，随后同bridge/InputRouter可创建fresh admission。

只有`skippable: true`的exact current pause可签发frozen zero-key、same-admission one-shot resume attempt。Attempt绑定exact
authority、direct-target proof、target、source revision、admitted frame与captured semantic port；`narrative.resume`先通过既有
Surface/input publication/physical gesture fence，continuation再按action mapping、attempt authority/kind/spent验证后一次消费，
重验current direct target、source、frame、semantic occurrence、`skippable`与port，并在最后一次direct proof后以captured exact
receiver/callable提交frozen `{ expectedOccurrenceId, resolution: { kind: "resume" } }`。Unmapped action、wrong attempt kind与
outer publication/gesture failure不消费authentic pause attempt；clone、foreign、repeat、replacement/retained、suspended或
disposed proof稳定zero-dispatch。Non-skippable pause的attempt issuance为`null`，raw/null attempt不能调用semantic port。

Semantic callable同步throw仍归一为rejected completion `Promise`；physical Surface receipt保持exact
`unchanged / surface.action_routed`，source-relative consumer result返回`dispatched`，且不会回滚或改写已经安装的composite
state。Successful semantic completion同样不乐观关闭root，后续仍由semantic publication驱动replace/empty。本批没有
实现say advance/auto、custom、presentation barrier、player-control semantic mapping或automatic controller-attempt/timer，也没有
接Narrative Host/History/React/live Story，未新增public/internal barrel、package export或generic receipt/code。

验证：focused `7 files / 192 tests`、UI package `79 files / 1010 tests`、全量
`253 files / 3938 tests`与完整`deno task check` green。本批未改变browser/build/live application graph，因此未机械重跑
browser、examples或prebuilt；最近已有的Engine browser `101 / 101`、examples `45 passed / 2 skipped`与prebuilt Player
`38 / 38`只作先前证据，不冒充本HEAD验证。该checkpoint的current曾为S4.1b.1b.1b.1，现由下述delivery取代。

### S4.1b.1b.1b.1 delivery — automatic pause-expiry controller attempt

同一个composition-owned stable action authority现新增source-relative ready-active target proof。它从whole-composite
topology与exact accepted baseline直接证明direct target、publisher lease、source revision、runtime instance、application
epoch、topology revision及`ready_instance / active` phase，却不把automatic eligibility误绑到physical input owner。
Capture与currentness均先执行registry/baseline/runtime全局coherence；legacy raw publisher-registry dispose造成的divergence会使
physical与automatic两类旧proof同时失效，fresh capture返回`surface.stable_reconcile_faulted`，valid physical route返回既有
`surface.transition_faulted`，且state identity与两类notification均保持零变化。该corrective没有新增generic result、receipt或
topology authority。

Narrative bridge-private controller claim复用上述authority与S4.1a已captured的exact semantic receiver/callable，不接受caller注入
kernel、lease、target或raw resolution。任意current direct ready+active Pause，无论`skippable`为`true`或`false`，都可签发
frozen zero-key automatic attempt；manual `narrative.resume`仍只由`skippable: true`控制。每个attempt绑定exact target/source/
frame、runtime/epoch/topology proof与controller generation；dispatch先one-shot spend，再重验current target/frame/port，构造
frozen `{ expectedOccurrenceId, resolution: { kind: "resume" } }`，最后一次proof通过后才调用captured exact receiver。
Callable同步throw只形成rejected completion `Promise`，不回滚或改写Surface state。

任意topology revision变化都会使旧attempt stale；若同一target在变化过程中始终ready+active，同一current generation可签发
fresh topology-bound attempt。若blocking preparation使root suspended、source/replacement/empty/publisher/Coordinator dispose使
generation不再current，state listener会撤销exact claim；恢复只能由S4.2建立fresh generation，旧controller的迟到dispose不能清除
successor claim。Clone、foreign、repeat、cross-generation与transition loser均zero-dispatch。10,000次controller create/issue/
dispose rotation不推进publisher source/occurrence、runtime identity或Surface notification；provenance只保留current opaque handle，
没有历史Set/array或数值generation cursor。

本floor不读取`presentationClock`、不创建timer，也不保存deadline、remaining duration/cursor或suspension scheduling；这些执行状态
仍由S4.2的`DialoguePlayerController`/Host拥有。Custom physical payload已重切为下一独立S4.1b.1b.1b.2a；
Say、presentation-barrier与player-control mapping仍保持stop并由S4.1b.1b.1b.2b裁决。本批保持source-relative/dormant；
没有Narrative Host、History child、React/Web/Story claimant、root或
`./internal` barrel、package export或live browser/build graph变化。

验证通过focused `7 files / 204 tests`、UI package `79 files / 1022 tests`、全量
`253 files / 3950 tests`与完整`deno task check` green。本批未机械重跑browser/examples/prebuilt；最近已有的Engine browser
`101 / 101`、examples `45 passed / 2 skipped`与prebuilt Player `38 / 38`只作先前证据，不冒充本HEAD验证。
S4.1b.1b.1b.1已完成；current/next现为
**S4.1b.1b.1b.2a custom physical payload admission**；该checkpoint现由下述delivery取代。

### S4.1b.1b.1b.2a delivery — custom physical payload admission

本批在既有source-relative、composition/Host-owned physical admission上完成`narrative.custom`的authenticated
physical-to-semantic vertical。Dormant harness中的admission只接受exact current ready+active custom frame的payload；S4.2 Host
后续只能向当次renderer交付绑定该admission的narrow callback。Payload先经Base
`parseInteractionResolutionV1` custom分支投影为detached、canonical-keyed、deep-frozen
`StrictJsonObjectV1`，再签发frozen zero-key one-shot attempt。Attempt只在private WeakMap中绑定exact admission authority、
direct-target proof、target、source revision、admitted frame、captured semantic port与该payload，不把raw receiver、callable、
envelope minting或generic semantic ingress交给Story renderer。

Payload traversal可能执行getter，因此attempt issuance在投影后重验active admission与exact claim，并重新capture exact current
target/source/contract/frame/proof；getter同步发布fresh occurrence会使outer issuance返回`null`，getter同步dispose并安装successor时
旧authority也不能签发token或清除successor claim。Authenticated route仍先完成Surface/input publication/physical gesture fence；
unmapped action与mapped cross-kind attempt在spend前返回，只有same-authority `narrative.custom` attempt会one-shot spend。随后再次
核对current target/source/frame/semantic port并完成最后一次direct-target proof，才以captured exact receiver/callable提交frozen
`{ expectedOccurrenceId, resolution: { kind: "custom", payload } }`。Clone、foreign、repeat、retained replacement、suspended、
publisher/admission dispose或stale gesture均不能触发semantic dispatch；sync throw规范化为rejected completion `Promise`，Story
rejection与throw均不回滚attempt spend或composite state。Narrative module在payload getter前捕获exact `Object.freeze`，并用它冻结
attempt、resolution、request与dispatched result，避免Base→UI handoff留下mutable action evidence。Base queue front继续按current occurrence、pending kind及Story-owned
`isCustomPayloadValid`执行最终业务校验，UI没有复制custom schema。

本批同时修复了Base既有bounded interaction JSON projector的cross-runtime dangerous-own-key缺口：`__proto__`、`constructor`与
`prototype`现在通过module-captured exact `Object.defineProperty`建立own enumerable data descriptor，并由module-captured
`Object.freeze`递归冻结；payload getter即使同步替换这两项global intrinsic也不能丢key、触发legacy setter、改变输出prototype或
留下可写结果。Deno与Node/browser
因此对该bounded interaction `StrictJsonObjectV1` direct-object输入收敛到相同own keys、descriptors与canonical bytes。这个corrective确实改变了此前在标准
Node/browser上错误投影dangerous `__proto__`的Base行为，不能表述为Base semantics zero diff；Base resolution union、evaluator、
queue contract、Save format以及ordinary maintained interaction bytes保持不变。

本delivery仍是dormant/source-relative seam：没有实现Say、presentation-barrier或player-control policy，没有接Narrative Host、
History、React/Web/live Story，也没有新增public/internal barrel、package export或generic Surface result/receipt。验证通过focused
`8 files / 216 tests`、UI package `79 files / 1028 tests`、全量`253 files / 3958 tests`、完整`deno task check`、fresh
Engine browser `101 / 101`、examples `45 passed / 2 skipped`与prebuilt Player `38 / 38`。S4.1b.1b.1b.2a已完成；
该checkpoint的current/next均为 **S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.1a physical Say reveal-first admission**；现由下述`.1a`
delivery推进。

**S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.0 policy adjudication delivery（docs-only）：** accepted mapping以
[design contract](../design/surface-contract-harness.md)的closed table为唯一authority：`ui.confirm`与
`narrative.advance`统一为Say reveal-first alias；`confirm`只禁止normal mode content-auto，`auto`在full reveal后
按`autoWaitMs`自动推进且允许manual first-win；Skip优先并保持既有seen policy。Barrier只接受exact Stage run proof，
`completed | skipped | interrupted`完成、`cancelled`不完成；fresh-generation `settle`不用重播，`replay`在没有
accepted capability时保持pending并报告`narrative.barrier_replay_unsupported`。Player mode是非持久化
`normal | auto | skip`，History只发exact child intent，voice replay只用current Say的captured optional callable；
`player.toggle_ui`从Narrative V1 catalog移除、隐藏能力defer，generic input ID本批不删除。

Stage terminal evidence绑定same-composition claim、opaque target identity与canonical pending bytes，明确排除
candidate snapshot/source/runtime；instant outcome可在单个O(1) current slot等待，dispatch时才绑定current
source/runtime/frame/semantic port；same-target readiness retry只在canonical pending bytes exact时复用，replacement/
empty/application successor/dispose删除。Fresh recovery generation只来自composition successor、accepted anchor epoch
rotation或initial coherent bootstrap。Reveal/timer只在ready-active运行；suspend保存integer cursor、sub-character remainder
与deadline remaining，resume使用fresh generation，且zero remaining也等next tick。Manual/auto/skip与Barrier各自共享
per-boundary in-flight claim；semantic completion必须先于bridge successor ordering证明后才可按exact unchanged source释放。
Voice unavailable仍是consumed family-local result；replay unsupported是once-per target/generation、zero-mutation
source-relative result。

本adjudication只修改两份owning design、focused plan与唯一cross-plan，没有source/test/runtime/live claimant，不更新live
architecture/features/development，也不记录unit/browser/build evidence。验证只要求四文档`deno fmt --check`与
`git diff --check`。本adjudication当时冻结的实现线性顺序为S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.1a → S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.1b →
S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.2a → S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.2b.0 → S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.2b.1 →

**S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.1 execution-order amendment（docs-only）：** `.1a`只在既有physical admission中交付
Host-owned exact reveal generation、两种Say alias、reveal-only exact frozen
`{ kind: "revealed", completion: null }`与complete-phase one-shot `advance`。Reveal port仅含descriptor-captured
own-data `capturePhaseInternalV1`/`revealAllInternalV1`，renderer只拿当次bound callback；入口phase只捕获一次，
同一事件不能reveal后再advance。Bridge-private per-current-Say-frame reveal controller独立于可替换的
`physicalActionAdmissionClaim`，拥有captured port与exact in-flight claim：source/frame/readiness/lifetime loss撤销，
active-only topology/InputRouter binding churn保留；physical admission只借用它，old admission/token不能影响fresh controller，
`.1b`也复用同一record。Issuance只绑定current identities、不读取phase，失败为attempt `null`/mint zero。Authenticated
route完成generic fence与mapping后取得controller boundary gate、验证currentness、spend，再调用一次`capturePhase`；callback
后先重验，drift的`stale/null`优先于invalid/throw的`faulted/null`。Incomplete再调用一次`revealAll`并post-recheck，drift为
stale、仍current的throw为faulted、success为`revealed/null`；semantic/in-flight均zero。只有complete row才把boundary
gate转为semantic in-flight。Captured semantic port的现有one-key
callable shape不变，但返回Promise必须在该dispatch触发的semantic publication与bridge reconcile drain完成后才settle，
Family再按exact unchanged source释放claim或随changed source退役。`.1b`只实现`advancePolicy: "auto"`的
ready-active、无gesturecontroller attempt，不读取clock、不建timer，也不实现player Auto/Skip。该amendment没有
source/test/runtime/live claimant或public/generic contract变更。

### S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.1a delivery — physical Say reveal-first admission

本批在既有唯一physical admission上交付`ui.confirm`与`narrative.advance`的Say-only alias，同时保留Choice、Pause与
Custom对这两个action的既有`unmapped`结果。Host reveal generation以exact two-key plain-data port进入；factory只捕获
own-data `capturePhaseInternalV1`/`revealAllInternalV1`的exact receiver/callables；该plain-data port本身不是opaque
provenance token，accessor、额外字段、foreign prototype、任一非callable member或重复claim均不能建立controller，
也不会烧掉bridge claim。Bridge-private per-current-Say-frame controller与可替换
`physicalActionAdmissionClaim`相互独立：admission重建可复用仍current的controller，controller successor也不能被old
admission/token清理或调用。

Attempt是frozen zero-key、same-admission/controller one-shot capability；issuance只绑定exact target/source/frame/semantic
port而不读取phase。Route先完成Surface/Input/publication/gesture与alias/token fence，再以bridge callback claim串行读取一次
captured phase。Callback后与`revealAll`后都重新验证exact admission/controller/target/source/frame；drift的`stale/null`
优先于invalid/throw的`faulted/null`。Incomplete只返回exact frozen`revealed/null`且semantic dispatch为零；complete才把
callback claim原子转换为semantic in-flight并提交frozen`{ expectedOccurrenceId, resolution: { kind: "advance" } }`。

Semantic in-flight claim与reveal-controller claim分离并以exact token CAS清理。Physical admission或controller重建不会释放
pending boundary；同帧blocking suspension撤销reveal generation但保留semantic claim与bounded lifecycle observer，Promise
drain完成后才释放。Source/frame/empty/lifetime drift会退休旧claim；old completion不能清除successor claim。Raw registry
divergence、retained/preparing/suspended target、stale gesture、callback reentry、clone/foreign/repeat与dispose均在读取phase或
semantic dispatch前fail closed。Controller/attempt provenance由WeakMap与单个current callback/in-flight slot保持有界，10k
reveal-only rotation不保留强attempt history或改变composite state/identity high-water。

本delivery仍是source-relative dormant floor：没有content-auto attempt、clock/timer/deadline、player Auto/Skip、Host/React/live
claimant，也没有public/internal barrel、package export或generic action/stable result扩张。验证通过focused
`7 files / 226 tests`、UI package `79 files / 1044 tests`、全量`253 files / 3974 tests`与完整`deno task check`。
本批未重跑browser/examples/prebuilt；Engine browser `101 / 101`、examples `45 passed / 2 skipped`与prebuilt Player
`38 / 38`仅是最近已有证据，不冒充本批HEAD验证。S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.1a已完成；current/next均为
**S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.1b content-auto Say controller-attempt floor**；该checkpoint现由下述entry amendment与delivery推进。

### S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.1b entry amendment — content-auto Say controller-attempt floor（docs-only）

本切片不建立第二个Say controller或bridge claim。现有exact per-current-frame
`NarrativeStableSayRevealControllerInternalV1`直接增加
`issueContentAutoAttemptInternalV1(): NarrativeStableSayContentAutoAttemptInternalV1 | null`与
`dispatchContentAutoInternalV1(attempt: unknown): NarrativeStableSayContentAutoDispatchResultInternalV1`。
Content-auto attempt是独立brand、frozen zero-key、WeakMap-authenticated one-shot capability；result是exact frozen
closed union：`{ kind: "dispatched", completion: Promise<unknown> } |
{ kind: "not_ready", completion: null } | { kind: "stale", completion: null } |
{ kind: "faulted", completion: null }`。这些名称与结果只存在于Narrative source-relative内部模块，不进入UI root、`./internal`
barrel、package export或generic Managed Surface result。

Issue保持phase-free：它只接受exact controller receiver、`advancePolicy: "auto"`的exact current Say frame、ready-active
controller generation、current target/source/frame/semantic port与fresh ready-active proof。它不读取
`capturePhaseInternalV1`，不调用`revealAllInternalV1`，也不依赖physical admission、InputRouter、input owner、gesture、clock
或timer。Confirm policy、inactive/suspended/retained/preparing controller、callback/semantic gate已占用、foreign receiver或
current-proof unspent auto attempt都返回`null`且mint zero。Active-only topology变化保留同一controller generation，但使旧proof/
attempt stale；同一generation可退休该旧attempt并用fresh proof签发新attempt。一个controller只强保留一个current auto
attempt，不保留attempt history。

Dispatch ordered contract固定为：exact controller receiver/lifetime → exact attempt provenance/current/unspent → ready-active
proof与target/source/frame/port/`advancePolicy`复验 → shared Say callback gate available → 原子claim gate、spend auto attempt并
退休同一frame的current manual与auto competitor → 只调用一次captured `capturePhaseInternalV1` → post-callback exact
controller/target/source/frame/port/readiness proof复验。Post-check drift固定返回`stale/null`且优先于callback outcome；仍exact
current的`incomplete`返回`not_ready/null`，throw或非`incomplete | complete`返回`faulted/null`，三者semantic dispatch均为
zero并释放callback gate。Auto path从不调用`revealAllInternalV1`。只有仍exact current的`complete`把同一callback gate原子
转成既有per-frame `saySemanticInFlightClaim`，完成最后proof check并提交frozen
`{ expectedOccurrenceId, resolution: { kind: "advance" } }`。

Manual与content-auto允许各自预签发一个attempt，但共用同一个bridge callback gate与semantic in-flight claim。先取得
callback gate的一方同步退休另一方的旧attempt；loser在winner的callback、semantic Promise pending及settlement以后都不能
复活。`not_ready`或`faulted`只允许重新签发fresh attempt，不恢复旧competitor。Physical reveal-only、manual complete与auto
complete也必须沿用同一first-wins helper/slot，不能复制一份平行semantic authority。

Lifecycle继续复用`.1a`：active-only topology churn保留controller、撤销旧attempt并允许fresh proof；blocking suspension立即
撤销controller与未提交attempt，resume只能建立fresh controller generation。若semantic dispatch已经开始，同帧suspend/
resume保留exact pending claim与bounded observer直到drain；source/frame replacement、empty、publisher/Coordinator dispose
则退休旧claim，old completion只能以exact CAS清理自身，不能清除successor。Semantic sync throw归一化为rejected Promise；
resolved/rejected completion都必须在该调用触发的semantic publication与同步Narrative bridge reconcile drain后、向caller
暴露settlement前完成exact claim/observer cleanup。无论manual还是auto，Promise pending期间全部competitor均为zero dispatch。

本entry amendment当时保持S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.1b为current/pending，不记录实现或验证证据；现由下述delivery关闭。它不读取clock，不创建timer/deadline/
remaining，不实现`autoWaitMs` scheduling、player Auto/Skip、Host/React/live claimant、Barrier、History或S4.2 controller。
若实现需要第二个Say lifecycle authority、raw renderer semantic port、timer/Host接线、generic/public result扩张，或不能证明
manual/auto共享gate与post-drain exact cleanup，立即停止并修订合同。

### S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.1b delivery — content-auto Say controller-attempt floor

现有per-current-frame `NarrativeStableSayRevealControllerInternalV1`已直接交付phase-free
`issueContentAutoAttemptInternalV1`与`dispatchContentAutoInternalV1`，没有建立第二个controller、bridge claim或semantic
authority。Issue只为`advancePolicy: "auto"`的exact current ready-active Say签发独立brand、frozen zero-key、WeakMap-authenticated
attempt；attempt保存exact controller claim、target/source/frame/semantic port与fresh ready-active proof。Confirm policy、inactive/
suspended/retained/preparing generation、occupied callback/semantic gate、foreign receiver、clone与current-proof duplicate全部
mint zero，且issue不读取phase、player profile、presentation clock、Input owner或gesture。

Dispatch按entry contract取得shared callback gate后spend exact attempt并同步退休同帧manual/auto competitor，再且只再读取一次
captured phase。Post-callback target/source/frame/readiness漂移固定为`stale/null`并优先于callback outcome；exact
`incomplete`返回`not_ready/null`，throw/invalid返回`faulted/null`，complete才把同一gate原子转换为既有per-frame semantic
in-flight claim并提交frozen `advance` request。四类result均为exact frozen Narrative-private shape；manual与auto双方winner都使
loser在callback、Promise pending及settlement后永久stale，`not_ready`或`faulted`也不会复活presigned manual token。Resolve、
reject与sync throw继续归一化为post-drain Promise completion，并在settlement暴露前以exact CAS清理claim/observer。

Active-only topology churn保留同一controller generation，但旧proof/attempt stale且可用fresh proof重新签发；较高
nonblocking input owner也不成为automatic admission前提。真实blocking suspension撤销未提交controller/attempt，resume需要fresh
generation；已提交semantic claim继续沿用`.1a`的same-frame suspend preservation。Source/frame successor退休旧claim，old
completion不能清除successor in-flight。Clone/foreign/wrong receiver在phase read前fail closed；single current attempt slot与
WeakMap provenance经10k `not_ready` rotation保持有界、state/notification/identity high-water为zero。

本delivery仍是source-relative dormant floor：没有读取clock、创建timer/deadline/remaining或实现`autoWaitMs` scheduling，
没有player Auto/Skip、Host/React/live claimant、Barrier、History，也没有UI root/`./internal` barrel、package export或generic
result扩张。验证通过focused `7 files / 236 tests`、UI package `79 files / 1054 tests`、full
`253 files / 3984 tests`与`deno task check` green。本批未重跑browser/examples/prebuilt；Engine browser `101 / 101`、
examples `45 passed / 2 skipped`与prebuilt Player `38 / 38`只作prior evidence，不冒充本批HEAD验证。

### S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.2 entry checkpoint — barrier acknowledgment/recovery（docs-only，已完成）

Barrier先拆为 **S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.2a normal Stage→Narrative acknowledgment** 与
**S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.2b settle/replay recovery**；后者现由`.2b.2b.0` docs-only generation admission与
`.2b.2b.1` implementation继续细分。`.2a`必须把exact Stage run与current
Narrative target的normal terminal path端到端闭合；`.2b`随后消费composition-owned fresh generation与
shared ingress，不能把React mount/effect伪装成recovery provenance。两批均保持source-relative、package-internal、
dormant且可独立合并；本checkpoint在实施前只冻结合同，不把当时的文档状态写成runtime delivery或验证证据。

`.2a`为每个exact Stage reconciler/Host generation只允许一个exact claimant通过
`claimStageAcknowledgedRunAuthorityInternalV1(reconciler, exactClaimant)`取得
`StageAcknowledgedRunAuthorityInternalV1` exclusive mutation authority；same claimant重复claim复用同一authority，
foreign/clone claimant fail closed。Claim后
`retarget`、`skipAll`、`suspend`、`resume`与`dispose`等全部Stage mutation entry只能经该authority；绕过facade的
raw writer与wrong receiver保持zero mutation。未claim的public `StageReconcilerV1`、public
`StageTransitionAcknowledgmentV1` shape、ordinary raw retarget与frame/subscription行为逐字保持，private proof不得从
public acknowledgment字段重建。Authority保留对应的`*InternalV1` mutation methods，并以
`retargetWithAcknowledgedRunInternalV1({ retarget, expectedTransitionId, commitGuard, terminalPort })`作为唯一
acknowledged-run入口；`StageAcknowledgedRunCommitGuardInternalV1`与`terminalPort`都必须descriptor-capture exact own-data
receiver/callable，两者的exact one-method shape分别是`isCommitCurrentInternalV1(): boolean`与
`deliverTerminalInternalV1({ proof, outcome }): void`，terminal input固定为frozen `{ proof, outcome }`。
同一authority另提供read-only
`isAcknowledgedRunTerminalStackActiveInternalV1(proof: unknown): boolean`；它只对same-authority、WeakMap-authenticated exact
proof返回private-terminal加其后public ack/diagnostic/subscription stack是否尚未退出，foreign、clone、unissued与已退出proof
均返回`false`且zero caller-field read。

Acknowledged retarget先在单一reentry fence内形成完整plan：从exact previous/next target与active-run snapshot推导change，
先模拟same-entry interruption suppression，再解析每个logical/original transition与effective reduced-motion behavior；在
current target/revision、active run、occurrence/proof、interrupt/start与notification发生任何mutation前，必须恰好一个
planned edge满足logical definition的`acknowledge === true`且`transitionId === expectedTransitionId`。零个、多个、
resolver/fallback callback throw、malformed plan或preflight reentry均保持上述Stage delta全部为零，且Stage-private result
固定为`{ kind: "armed", proof } | { kind: "stale", proof: null } |
{ kind: "faulted", code, proof: null }`。零个使用
`stage.acknowledged_run_unmatched`，多个使用`stage.acknowledged_run_ambiguous`，planning/callback/provenance fault使用
`stage.acknowledged_run_faulted`；不得在fault后调用raw retarget降级继续。`proof`是frozen zero-key、WeakMap-authenticated
capability。

Stage在full plan完成后先调用一次exact `commitGuard`；若随后中断old armed runs，每一个old run的
private terminal、public acknowledgment/diagnostic与Stage subscription callbacks全部完成后、new
occurrence/proof/target/run commit前都必须再调用同一guard。Guard返回`false`直接得到上述`stale`并停止
中断余下old runs；已发生的old interruption/notification不回滚，但new Stage delta仍为零。Throw、invalid return或
reentry得到`stage.acknowledged_run_faulted`；最后一次guard返回`true`后到new occurrence/proof/target/run assignment之间不得
再执行caller callback、property lookup或其他可抛工作。这样Narrative current target/canonical/generation检查由
source-relative controller在Stage fence内完成，Stage本身不读取Narrative；单纯two-phase plan token不足以覆盖
old-run terminal callback的同步reentry，因此不得用它替代此final commit guard。
任意full-plan/guard/old-terminal/public-observer callback里的nested same-authority mutation都zero delta并标记outer；后续
guard false时`stale`优先，否则outer为`stage.acknowledged_run_faulted`。

Proof绑定claim authority、exact reconciler/Host generation、current presentation epoch、logical expected transition与exact
effective run occurrence。
Reduced-motion fallback即使使用不同effective transition ID，仍以logical/original ID完成
matching；cut、zero-duration、reduced-motion instant settle与fallback也分配exact opaque effective run occurrence，并在
acknowledged-retarget返回前把同步`completed`写入current terminal slot。Animated与
asset-readiness run在
start/readiness hold前已完成proof binding。Public acknowledgment继续保持既有字段与logical/effective public行为，不成为
private admission oracle。

Private terminal callback必须先于全部public Stage frame subscriber、acknowledgment与diagnostic callback取得exact
outcome，并隔离后者的throw；claimed run的terminal PresentationRun notification不得在private delivery前透传，public
callback不得阻断private evidence、复活run或造成半个arm。Unclaimed ordinary public path保持existing behavior。若valid outer
retarget先按plan中断old armed run，old
`interrupted`/`cancelled` callback可同步first-win；callback若reenter并改变Stage/Narrative/generation exact precondition，
outer Narrative retarget结果必须为`stale`且不得提交new Stage target/proof/run，已经完成的old interruption不回滚。
Repeat/late/foreign run receipt均为
zero proof、zero Narrative mutation。

Narrative首次接受`presentation_barrier` target时分配一个opaque target-level evidence identity；它绑定publisher/target
occurrence、semantic occurrence与full normalized `PendingInteractionV1` canonical bytes，而不绑定source revision、runtime
instance、candidate snapshot或semantic port。Readiness-failed same-target retry只有canonical bytes exact时保留该identity；
fresh source/frame/port/runtime只在每次dispatch issuance时重新捕获。Preparing、blocking suspended与exact
`readiness_failed` gap可保存一个current terminal slot，但不能提交semantic resolution；replacement、semantic occurrence或
canonical bytes变化、empty、application/presentation successor、Stage/Narrative dispose均同步retire slot、attempt与observer。

Narrative同批建立source-relative `NarrativeStableBarrierAcknowledgmentControllerInternalV1`；factory从bridge private
provenance自行claim同一composite kernel的stable ready-active authority与Stage authority，不接受caller传入raw authority。
Controller只暴露current Barrier acknowledged retarget、
`flushRetainedTerminalInternalV1(): NarrativeStableBarrierTerminalDispatchResultInternalV1 | null`与dispose。其Stage
retarget result固定为
`armed { completion: null } | stale { completion: null } | faulted { code:
"stage.acknowledged_run_unmatched" | "stage.acknowledged_run_ambiguous" |
"stage.acknowledged_run_faulted", completion: null }`；Stage `stale`由wrapper原样映射，且只表示Narrative target/canonical/
generation precondition已经失效，不能冒充Stage planning fault。

Stage outcome `completed`、`skipped`与`interrupted`形成eligible terminal evidence；`cancelled`terminal-seal exact run且
永远zero semantic dispatch。Terminal callback先不可逆spend exact proof，再按application/target/canonical currentness分类，但只把evidence/
cancelled result写入唯一O(1) target-level slot，不在Stage terminal call stack内调用semantic port。后续显式flush才尝试
semantic admission；preparing、suspended与failure gap上的eligible evidence形成retained result，pending期间repeat flush复用
same exact cached dispatched result且zero new dispatch，无current evidence/result时返回`null`。Exact frozen terminal result union固定为
`dispatched { completion: Promise<unknown> } | retained { completion: null } |
cancelled { completion: null } | stale { completion: null } | faulted { completion: null }`；retained只有exact eligible
evidence尚不能在current ready-active runtime dispatch时成立，stale/faulted不得降级为retained或cancelled。
Public Stage callback内的nested flush必须因上述proof-bound terminal-stack状态返回`retained`且semantic zero；Stage terminal调用
返回后同栈的explicit flush已处于stack外并可立即继续admission，不得以microtask gate延迟或要求Host猜测retry。

每次eligible flush签发fresh frozen zero-key attempt，绑定current target-level evidence、fresh source/frame/semantic port/
runtime ready-active proof与controller generation。Final currentness check后，attempt在调用semantic port前不可逆spend并占用
该barrier boundary唯一in-flight claim；normal acknowledgment、settle recovery、repeat callback与reentrant observer共用此
first-wins gate。Sync throw规范化为rejected completion Promise，不回滚attempt、terminal evidence或Surface state。Promise
settlement依赖既有semantic-port drain合同：只有该调用触发的publication与Narrative reconcile已同步drain后才可向caller暴露；
old completion仅按exact claim CAS清理，不能ABA-clear successor。In-flight token属于target-level evidence，不属于
source/frame/controller；Semantic completion pending期间全部competitor均为zero，source/frame successor可安装但不能取得
第二claim。Completion对应的publication与Narrative reconcile drain完成后，CAS先清理exact token；若
target/semantic occurrence/canonical pending仍current，只恢复retained state，由后续显式flush重新捕获fresh
source/frame/port/proof，不在settlement stack里自动重试。若target-level evidence已退役，completion只清理bounded
tombstone而不复活。

#### S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.2b.0 generation admission amendment（docs-only，历史 checkpoint）

Recovery generation是bridge/application-scoped authority，不是current target、React mount或controller object的生命周期。
`NarrativeStableBarrierAcknowledgmentControllerInternalV1`因此允许在current target为empty或非Barrier时由
composition构造；该controller仍是同一bridge上唯一exact Stage claimant，而`.2a`的retarget/flush在没有
exact current Barrier时仍只返回既定stale/null。Generation、prior Stage proof、preexisting target-or-null、
recovery attempt/cache与bounded observer都存于bridge record；controller dispose/recreate不得丢失或重新采样它们。
Fresh application successor使用fresh bridge、Stage claimant与activation-gate binding，因而自然进入fresh domain，不新增
monotonic cursor或anchor-token authority。

Stage authority新增frozen zero-key `StagePresentationGenerationProofInternalV1`及prior-proof relation capture。
`captureCurrentPresentationGenerationInternalV1(previousProof)`只从accepted current Stage epoch产生source-relative、
WeakMap-authenticated result：`{ kind: "captured", relation: "initial" | "equal" | "higher", proof } |
{ kind: "stale", proof: null } | { kind: "faulted", proof: null }`。`previousProof === null`只能得到`initial`；非null prior必须属于
same exact authority/reconciler domain，再以其authenticated epoch与current accepted epoch产生关系。Equal epoch在同一
reconciler/authority上复用exact proof relation；数字epoch相同但reconciler/authority已替换是equal-foreign，必须`faulted`，
不能因React remount、StrictMode或callback/effect identity变化伪造`equal`或freshness。Disposed/no-current Stage为
`stale`；current accepted epoch低于authenticated prior也直接为`stale`，不产生captured lower proof。Clone、foreign、
malformed prior、receiver/capture throw或内部provenance divergence为`faulted`。Stage proof不暴露
raw epoch、target或public acknowledgment字段。若acknowledged-run planning/interrupting operation或其他authority mutation仍在
调用栈内，capture进入既有reentry fence并返回`faulted`，不得读取、mint或替换generation proof。

Stage claim后public reconciler mutation已经封闭，因此higher/lower epoch不得靠raw public retarget、test hook或第二writer制造。
同一个`StageAcknowledgedRunAuthorityInternalV1`新增
`retargetPresentationGenerationInternalV1(retarget: StageRetargetInputV1)`与exact frozen
`StagePresentationGenerationRetargetResultInternalV1 = { kind: "retargeted" } | { kind: "stale" } |
{ kind: "faulted" }`。该方法只在initial Stage bootstrap或`retarget.epoch !== current accepted epoch`时走既有contained
epoch-replacement路径，静默dispose old runs并安装stable target/revision/epoch；same epoch与disposed返回`stale`且全部Stage mutation/
notification zero，acknowledged planning/interrupting、nested authority mutation或callback reentry返回`faulted`。Existing Barrier
controller增加exact wrapper `retargetPresentationStageInternalV1(retarget)`，只在controller/bridge claim current且
`stageRetargetInProgress`为空时委托该方法并透传result；它与normal acknowledged retarget共用同一Stage authority/fence，不读取或
修改recovery generation。Higher/lower测试先经这个真实wrapper推进Stage，再显式synchronize，禁止直接访问raw authority。
Initial/higher/lower retarget与same-epoch stale均不得读取、清除或替换Narrative target-level
`barrierTargetTerminalClaim`、callback/semantic-in-flight claim或Promise tombstone；lower随后synchronize为stale时，old completion仍
保持exact CAS ownership，competitor继续zero dispatch。
Claim后的既有`stageAuthority.retargetInternalV1(retarget)`同时收窄为只接受already-initialized、same-current-epoch ordinary
retarget；uninitialized或epoch mismatch必须Stage target/run/revision/notification exact zero。Initial/higher/lower只经
`retargetPresentationGenerationInternalV1`，normal acknowledged Barrier只经`retargetWithAcknowledgedRunInternalV1`，避免同一claimed
authority内部保留第二条generation writer。

Narrative的`synchronizeRecoveryGenerationInternalV1(activationGate)`不接受caller-supplied epoch/target，只把bridge保留的
prior proof交给已claim的exact Stage authority，并返回下列frozen exact union：
`{ kind: "installed", generation } | { kind: "unchanged", generation } |
{ kind: "stale", generation: null } | { kind: "faulted", generation: null }`。
Stage `equal`只在supplied `activationGate`的object identity与bridge存储的exact gate receiver相同时返回
`unchanged`，并复用exact generation、prior proof、preexisting capture与cached result identity；此处只比较object
identity，不读取gate property/callable、current target、semantic port或notification。Equal但gate identity不同映射
`faulted`，且对该foreign gate的property为zero-read。Stage lower/noncurrent/unavailable的`stale`映射Narrative
`stale`，foreign/malformed/equal-new-reconciler/capture fault映射`faulted`；两者都必须在gate或target读取前
返回，且generation/result/attempt/observer与notification为zero delta。Existing generation与prior proof不同步、
`initial`但bridge已有generation、或`equal/higher`却没有exact prior generation都是provenance divergence，稳定
`faulted`。

Initial/higher只能在本次synchronize参数的source-relative exact shared
`ManagedSurfaceFamilyActivationGateInternalV1` data-callable port通descriptor-safe capture，且其exact receiver/callable返回
strict `false`时安装。Gate只接受exact-key plain own-data callable；accessor、extra field、foreign prototype、
non-callable member、wrong receiver、throw或non-boolean result均`faulted`。该source-relative port不是public opaque provenance
token，不承诺对一个内容相同的plain clone作身份识别；Host只能传入composition当次exact gate且不得外泄、
伪造或从React lifecycle重建该port。Initial/higher安装后把本次exact gate receiver/callable存入bridge generation；
higher允许在closed successor transaction提供fresh exact gate，而controller dispose/recreate在equal path上必须传回stored
gate object。Relation已认证后，
transaction只采样当时exact current Barrier的target、semantic occurrence、canonical pending bytes与
`loadRecovery`，或冻结`preexisting: null`；随后按顺序重验controller/bridge claim、same Stage proof/current
relation、captured gate receiver/callable仍为本次exact binding且closed、target-or-null仍exact，最后才以一次
no-throw local assignment安装fresh
generation。Gate已open、Stage/target/currentness drift返回`stale`；gate callback throw/non-boolean、target capture throw或
reentrant/divergent relation返回`faulted`；任一失败都不得部分撤销old generation。Higher成功后只退役old
generation-local attempt/result/observer；已占用的target-level `barrierTargetTerminalClaim`、callback/
semantic-in-flight claim与Promise tombstone继续由`.2a` exact CAS清理，不得被generation rotation或old completion
ABA-clear。

Generation安装时冻结的`preexisting: null`以及同generation稍后才发布的Barrier都没有recovery资格，
后者仍须走`.2a`真实Stage proof。Preexisting `loadRecovery: "settle"`只在exact gate已open、same
generation/target identity、current ready-active target/source/frame/semantic-port/runtime proof全部current时签发
`NarrativeStableBarrierRecoveryAttemptInternalV1`。Issuance不抢占global Barrier terminal claim；dispatch在one-shot
spend前完成generation/target/source/gate/runtime重验，再与`.2a`共用existing
`barrierTargetTerminalClaim`、callback/semantic-in-flight first-wins gate、Promise drain与exact CAS。Normal Stage
terminal先占claim则recovery stale/zero；recovery先占claim则后续Stage commit guard stale/zero。Sync throw仍只形成
rejected completion Promise，不回滚attempt spend、claim或Surface state；same canonical target的readiness/source retry只能在
old Promise drain后重新签发fresh proof-bound attempt。

Preexisting `loadRecovery: "replay"`不签发attempt，也不占用global Barrier terminal/in-flight claim。
`readReplayRecoveryUnsupportedInternalV1()`只在exact current preexisting target/generation上以独立generation-local
once-per-target claim返回并缓存唯一frozen
`{ kind: "unsupported", code: "narrative.barrier_replay_unsupported", completion: null }`。Equal generation/repeat复用同一
identity且notification、semantic、source、runtime、topology全部zero；只有fresh installed generation可建立fresh identity，
successor target必须由该fresh generation捕获为preexisting，same-generation target successor保持ineligible；lower/foreign/late
old generation不得读取或复活old result。Replay unsupported与settle使用同一bounded
generation-local recovery claim domain，但unsupported不伪造Stage proof、semantic dispatch或已实现replay capability。

`.2a` RED至少覆盖：unclaimed public exact behavior、exclusive claim与全部raw mutation bypass；full-plan exact-one的0/1/2
matching、suppression、resolver/fallback throw与nested reentry；plan后guard false/throw/invalid、old terminal reentry后second guard及
final guard到assignment zero read/throw；logical original ID对different fallback ID；animated、asset wait、
instant/cut/reduced settle；completed/skipped/interrupted/cancelled、old-interruption first-win与foreign same-ID run；terminal早于
public subscriber/ack/diagnostic、各observer throw隔离、terminal-port throw seal；Surface readiness、preparing/suspended/failure-gap
retention、same-target retry/fresh port、replacement/empty/epoch/dispose；
duplicate/late receipt、Promise pending competitor、sync throw/reject、post-drain CAS与10k bounded target/run churn。

`.2b.2b.0/.2b.2b.1` RED必须以mutation-sensitive exact rows覆盖：

- empty、non-Barrier与preexisting settle/replay三种initial bootstrap；empty/null capture后的same-generation late
  Barrier不获得资格；
- Stage previous proof为`null`、repeated equal capture复用exact same proof identity（不冻结capture result object identity）、higher、lower、disposed/no-current、
  clone/foreign/malformed及equal numeric epoch + new reconciler/authority；
- presentation-generation retarget的initial/higher/lower、same-epoch stale、disposed stale、planning/interrupting/nested callback
  fault、exact controller receiver/claim、claimed `retargetInternalV1`对uninitialized/epoch mismatch exact zero与raw public
  reconciler仍封闭；higher/lower relation只由该wrapper建立；
- `initial/higher`的fresh closed gate、open/drift/throw/non-boolean/reentry、higher fresh-gate replacement、target
  getter/capture drift、final postcheck与no-throw assignment；`equal`只接受stored exact gate object且对其property/
  target getter为zero-read，different gate identity为`faulted`；`lower/foreign`对gate/target getter为exact zero-read；
- controller在empty target上构造、duplicate claim、dispose/recreate exact generation复用、foreign gate/reconciler、
  accessor/extra/foreign-prototype/noncallable/wrong-receiver gate、fresh application bridge/domain与old proof/attempt/result
  不复活；plain data-callable clone只按当次initial/higher capture值验证，不伪装成opaque provenance rejection；
- preexisting-only settle issuance、gate-open、ready-active、preparing/suspended/readiness-failed gap、active resume、
  same-target source/frame/port retry、replacement/semantic/canonical drift、empty/publisher/application dispose；
- normal Stage terminal first、recovery dispatch first、Stage callback/observer reentry、attempt clone/foreign/repeat/spent、
  semantic sync throw/reject、pending Promise cached result、source successor/tombstone、old normal/recovery Promise仍持有global
  tombstone时的higher-generation与lower-generation retarget、post-drain fresh retry与old completion ABA；
- replay unsupported不签发attempt、不占global terminal/in-flight claim，exact target/equal-generation repeat复用同一
  result identity，fresh installed generation只为其捕获的preexisting successor target建立fresh identity，same-generation target
  successor与lower/foreign/late generation读取为null，10,000次repeat/
  rotation只保留O(1) current generation/attempt/cache/observer且Surface/source/runtime/topology/semantic/family
  notification全部zero；
- existing headless trusted path、public `StageReconcilerV1`、acknowledgment shape、Base parser/evaluator/queue与
  package export/barrel inventory unchanged。

`.2b.2b.0`只修订design/active plan，不交付source/test/runtime或验证数字；`.2b.2b.1`才实现上述
Stage relation capture、bridge generation owner、settle/replay behavior与tests。两个切片都不实现真实Barrier replay、
Stage/Narrative React Host、live Story claimant、timer/player mode/History、public或`./internal` barrel、package export、generic
result/receipt、Base interaction/Save/Persistence/canonical/digest/replay/wire变化。若controller仍必须保持target-scoped而无法
在empty bootstrap冻结`preexisting: null`、exact closed gate与Stage prior-proof relation无法在target/result mutation前完成双重重验、
freshness只能借React lifecycle/effect identity或cloneable public acknowledgment建立、normal/recovery需要第二个Barrier semantic
authority、或实现要求提前接入generic composition/Host/public contract，立即停止并修订design/active plan。

### S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.2a delivery — normal Stage→Narrative acknowledgment

`.2a`已交付exact Stage reconciler/Host generation上的claim-exclusive、package-internal
`StageAcknowledgedRunAuthorityInternalV1`。未claim的ordinary public reconciler与frame/subscription/acknowledgment
shape保持既有行为；claim后所有Stage mutation只能经同一authority，raw writer、foreign claimant、clone与wrong receiver均
fail closed。Acknowledged retarget只形成一次full plan，并在任何new mutation前验证exact-one logical transition ID；零个、多个及
planning/callback/provenance fault分别保持exact Stage zero delta。Narrative提供descriptor-captured commit guard；Stage在full plan后及
每个old-run terminal/public-observer stack退出后重验，最后一次`true`到new occurrence/proof/target/run assignment之间没有caller
callback或可抛读取。Opaque proof在run readiness/start前完成绑定；private terminal先于public acknowledgment、diagnostic与subscriber
不可逆seal，public observer throw及deferred clock rearm failure不能回滚claimed run或形成Stage/Narrative分叉。

Stage authority的proof-bound `isAcknowledgedRunTerminalStackActiveInternalV1`只认证same-authority exact proof，Narrative因此不使用
microtask或Host retry猜测terminal stack。`NarrativeStableBarrierAcknowledgmentControllerInternalV1`从bridge private provenance
自行claim Stage与composition stable-action authority；Stage terminal port只把`completed | skipped | interrupted`保存为eligible
target-level evidence，`cancelled`terminal-seal且semantic zero。Semantic port只由显式
`flushRetainedTerminalInternalV1()`触发：private/public terminal callback中的nested flush稳定返回`retained`，Stage terminal调用返回后
immediate explicit flush即可进入ready-active admission；instant/cut、animated、asset wait与reduced-motion fallback共享同一规则。

Evidence只绑定exact admitted target、semantic occurrence与full normalized pending canonical bytes。Preparing、真实blocking
suspension及`readiness_failed` gap保留eligible evidence而不dispatch；resume后可fresh flush。Same-target retry复用exact target-level
evidence，但dispatch重新捕获fresh source/frame/semantic port/runtime proof，旧port保持zero。Replacement、empty、epoch/publisher/
controller dispose同步retire current evidence与observer。每个Barrier boundary只有一个callback/semantic-in-flight claim；Promise pending
期间repeat与competitor均zero new dispatch并复用cached result。Source/frame successor可安装，但旧claim作为bounded tombstone继续阻挡
fresh proof；drain后只以exact token CAS清理并允许显式fresh retry，old completion、late receipt、foreign/clone proof与dispose/recreate
均不能ABA-clear successor claim。Target/run 10k churn保持O(1) current evidence、observer与provenance。

本delivery仍是source-relative、package-internal dormant floor：没有recovery generation、settle/replay recovery、真实Barrier replay、
Stage/Narrative React Host、live Story claimant、timer/player mode/History，也没有public或`./internal` barrel、package export、generic
Surface result/receipt、Base interaction、Save/Persistence/canonical/digest/replay/wire变化。验证通过focused
`8 files / 292 tests`、UI package `79 files / 1099 tests`、full `253 files / 4029 tests`与`deno task check` green；fresh
Engine browser `101 / 101`、examples `45 passed / 2 skipped`、prebuilt Player `38 / 38`均green。
S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.2a完成时active current/next均为
**S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.2b.1 settle/replay recovery implementation**；该checkpoint现已由下述
delivery关闭。

### S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.2b.1 delivery — settle/replay recovery implementation

Stage的exact claimed authority现以
`retargetPresentationGenerationInternalV1`作为initial/higher/lower presentation generation的唯一writer，
并以source-relative、WeakMap-authenticated proof捕获prior/current epoch relation。Same epoch与disposed为
stale，current低于authenticated prior也不生成lower proof；clone、foreign authority/reconciler、malformed proof与
nested mutation/callback reentry均fail closed。Claim后的ordinary `retargetInternalV1`只允许already-initialized
same-current-epoch mutation，generation retarget、acknowledged retarget、capture与skip/ordinary mutation共用同一
authority-mutation fence，因而不保留第二条generation writer或public raw bypass。

Narrative bridge现允许在empty或non-Barrier target上构造controller，并由bridge/application lifetime独占
prior Stage proof、current recovery generation、preexisting target-or-null、exact activation-gate binding、attempt/cache与
bounded observer。Controller dispose/recreate不会重新采样或丢失这些状态；bridge dispose则在composite
disposal notification前先关闭family ingress。Synchronization对exact gate receiver/callable作descriptor-safe
capture与pre/post currentness复验：equal只对stored exact gate返回unchanged，higher只在fresh closed gate上
原子安装successor，lower或unavailable Stage返回stale。Gate/target drift、foreign binding或nested
synchronize/retarget会使outer transaction fault，并保留完整predecessor generation；已占用的target-level
claim与Promise tombstone不随generation旋转被清除。

Settle recovery只为该generation安装时捕获的preexisting `loadRecovery: "settle"` Barrier签发
fresh frozen one-shot attempt；`preexisting: null`与same-generation后发Barrier保持ineligible。Issuance/dispatch绑定
exact generation/target、open gate、current ready-active source/frame/semantic port与runtime proof；controller不存在时
bridge-owned observer仍会对readiness、source或frame drift同步退役stale attempt。Normal acknowledgment与recovery
共用Barrier target/callback/semantic first-win gate与Promise tombstone exact CAS；semantic sync throw规范化为
rejected completion，不回滚spent attempt/claim，old completion不能ABA-clear successor。Preexisting
`loadRecovery: "replay"`只返回并复用exact generation/target的frozen
`narrative.barrier_replay_unsupported` result identity，不签发attempt、不占global terminal claim且semantic zero；
fresh installed generation才能建立fresh cached identity。10,000次generation/attempt/replay rotation保持O(1)
current proof、attempt、cache与observer。

本delivery仍是source-relative、package-internal dormant floor：不实现真实Barrier replay、Stage/Narrative React Host、
live Story claimant、timer/player mode或History，不变更UI root/`./internal` barrel、package export、public acknowledgment或
generic Surface result/receipt，也不改动Base interaction、Save/Persistence、canonical/digest/replay/wire contract。
验证通过focused `8 files / 315 tests`、UI package `79 files / 1122 tests`、full `253 files / 4052 tests`与
`deno task check`；fresh Engine browser `101 / 101`、examples `45 passed / 2 skipped`与prebuilt Player
`38 / 38`均green。S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.2b.1已完成；active current/next与唯一implementation gate现均为
**S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.3 player controls**；该聚合checkpoint现由下述`.3.0` amendment取代。

   ready-active Dialogue parent proof通过后读取descriptor-captured read-only History availability。Unavailable返回consumed

### S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.3b.0 entry amendment — exact voice-replay route contract（已完成，历史 checkpoint）

原 **S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.3b voice replay physical route** 标记为superseded checkpoint；本docs-only
entry冻结exact contract；实际source/test切片
**S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.3b.1 voice replay physical route implementation**现已由下述delivery履行。

本entry使用以下closed、package-internal名称，`.3b.1`不得另造平行authority或把raw adapter暴露给renderer：

- `NarrativeStableVoiceReplayPortInternalV1`是candidate preflight接收的raw optional port。非`null` receiver必须是
  non-array plain object、prototype exact `Object.prototype`、own keys exact only
  `replayCurrentVoiceInternalV1`，且该key必须是own data descriptor callable `(): boolean`。Array/function、null-prototype/
  foreign-prototype object、extra string或symbol key、accessor、inherited/missing/non-callable descriptor及prototype/ownKeys/
  descriptor trap/throw均使整个candidate稳定归`narrative.candidate_preflight_faulted`，且source/occurrence/runtime/topology/
  notification allocation为零。
- `NarrativeStableCapturedVoiceReplayPortInternalV1`是preflight建立的frozen zero-key branded handle；private
  WeakMap只保存exact raw receiver与该次descriptor-captured callable。Admitted frame只保存该handle或`null`，route
  不持有可调用raw identity，不重新读取caller property。Raw callable随后被替换、删除或改成accessor也不能改变已接受
  frame；调用时zero arguments且`this`必须仍是exact captured receiver。
- `NarrativeStableVoiceReplayActionAttemptInternalV1`是frozen zero-key、one-shot authenticated physical attempt；
  它绑定issuing admission、current stable input contract、现有`ManagedSurfaceStableDirectActionTargetProofInternalV1`、exact
  target、source revision、admitted frame与captured voice handle或`null`，不新增ready-active-proof type，也不携带raw
  receiver/callable、action spelling或可复制target字段。Ready-active由current route/input/direct-target/frame复验共同证明。
- `NarrativeStableVoiceReplayDispatchResultInternalV1`是exact closed union：
  `{ kind: "handled", completion: null } | { kind: "ignored", completion: null } |
  { kind: "stale", completion: null } | { kind: "faulted", completion: null }`。四个result均frozen；它只进入
  既有`NarrativeStablePhysicalActionDispatchResultInternalV1`的Narrative-local union及authenticated consumer result，不扩张
  generic `ManagedSurfaceActionRouteResultV1`、Input receipt/code或Surface receipt/code。

Voice replay必须复用`NarrativeStablePublisherBridgeRecordInternalV1`既有bridge-wide `sayCallbackClaim`，不得新增
`voiceReplayCallbackClaim`或第二callback authority。Generic authenticated route的`routeInProgress`只约束一个binding/claim；
它不能阻止callback同步dispose old admission、在same frame建立fresh binding/admission后从successor reenter。Issue要求
`bridgeRecord.sayCallbackClaim === null`且`bridgeRecord.saySemanticInFlightClaim === null`；route则在authentic voice attempt先spend并完成
currentness复验。Captured handle为`null`时直接返回`ignored`且不安装任何claim；只有non-null binding才重验这两个shared claim，
并由winner以exact identity CAS把own claim安装到`sayCallbackClaim`。该claim存在期间，existing manual Say与content-auto路径
沿用其既有检查而不能issue/dispatch，same-frame successor也不能mint fresh voice attempt或进入第二次voice callback。
Outer release/finally只在`bridgeRecord.sayCallbackClaim === ownClaim`时清理；old route绝不能ABA-clear successor claim或
successor admission；physical admission dispose也不得清理或转移该claim，只有持有own token的outer finally可以exact-CAS
release。Voice claim永不转移到`bridgeRecord.saySemanticInFlightClaim`。本切片不新增callback lifecycle observer或ready-active-proof type；
existing direct-target proof、exact route/input/frame pre/post capture与既有shared Say claim是唯一voice lifecycle authority。

`issueVoiceReplayAttemptInternalV1()`只在admission仍current、existing `bridgeRecord.sayCallbackClaim`与
`bridgeRecord.saySemanticInFlightClaim`均为空，且exact current frame为ready-active `say`时返回attempt；availability不取决于voice port
是否为`null`，因为absent是可路由的`ignored` outcome。
Current ready-active Say的`advancePolicy: "confirm" | "auto"`都可issue。Choice、pause、custom、barrier、empty、preparing、
retained replacement、suspended、exiting、publisher/bridge/admission/coordinator disposed均不可issue；其中barrier/empty沿用既有
physical-admission construction unavailable，其他kind的live admission调用该method返回`null`。Issue不调用voice callback，
也不读取semantic dispatch port或形成semantic resolution。Authentic attempt可按不同真实gesture重复签发，但每个identity只可
被一个winning route spend。

Exact route precedence固定如下，`.3b.1`测试必须以mutation方式证明顺序而非只测最终kind：

1. 先完整通过existing generic envelope/application/terminal/coherence、topology/instance/input owner/routing lease/action
   catalog、input publication与gesture fences。任一generic fence失败时consumer保持`null`，voice callback为零且传入attempt
   不spend；Input/Surface receipt沿用generic既有合同。
2. Consumer只把exact `player.replay_voice`映射到voice replay。Unmapped/wrong action或未进入consumer的unpublished action
   不得spend authentic voice attempt。
3. Exact voice mapping先认证attempt identity：必须是same admission、correct `voice_replay` kind且unspent；spoof、clone、
   foreign-admission、wrong-kind或already-spent attempt closed为`stale`且callback为零，并不得spend任何其他authentic attempt。
   一旦authentic same-admission correct-kind unspent voice attempt进入consumer，就必须立即one-shot spend；spend发生在current
   proof/frame/handle/callback-claim precheck之前，后续任何family-local `handled | ignored | stale | faulted`都不回滚spent。
4. Spend之后按`bridgeRecord.physicalActionAdmissionClaim === admissionClaim` → current direct-target proof → current stable input
   contract/direct target/source revision → exact admitted frame且`pending.kind === "say"` → frame内captured handle identity顺序复验；
   route/input/direct-target/frame共同证明ready-active。Proof/capture throw或任一漂移均为`stale`且不调用callback。Captured handle为
   `null`时不读取raw value、不安装`sayCallbackClaim`并直接返回`ignored`。只有non-null binding才继续要求existing
   `bridgeRecord.sayCallbackClaim`与`bridgeRecord.saySemanticInFlightClaim`均为空并CAS安装own
   shared claim；claim conflict为spent `stale`。CAS后必须再次重验exact physical-admission claim、direct-target proof、stable input
   contract、target/source/frame/handle及`bridgeRecord.saySemanticInFlightClaim === null`；post-claim drift为spent `stale`并exact-CAS release，
   callback为零。仍current的winner才以exact captured receiver/callable同步调用一次。Nested
   `issueVoiceReplayAttemptInternalV1()`必须返回`null`；same-binding nested route沿用generic
   `ui.managed_surface_action_route_in_progress`，manual/content-auto reentry由shared `sayCallbackClaim`拒绝，old-admission dispose +
   same-frame successor binding也不得绕过该claim。
5. 无论callback返回还是throw，先执行postcheck：`bridgeRecord.sayCallbackClaim === ownClaim`、
   `bridgeRecord.physicalActionAdmissionClaim === admissionClaim`、admission active、bridge lifecycle、exact current route/input/
   direct-target proof仍共同证明ready-active，且target/source/frame与captured handle必须全部仍相同。Target/source/frame replacement、root suspension、
   bridge/admission/coordinator dispose、successor admission或claim drift一律以`stale`覆盖callback的`true`、`false`、invalid return
   或throw；随后按exact CAS只释放own claim。
6. Non-null binding只有postcheck仍current时才分类callback：captured callable返回exact `true`为`handled`，返回exact
   `false`为`ignored`，同步throw或非boolean return为`faulted`；port absent已在step 4直接`ignored`。Throw不向route caller传播；
   四类outcome均已spend attempt，repeat route为`stale`，claim释放后fresh gesture可签发fresh attempt。

Authenticated `player.replay_voice`通过generic gate后，`handled | ignored | stale | faulted`四类family result的Input均为
`consumed / input.managed_surface_consumed`，Surface均保持既有`unchanged / surface.action_routed`，lower context不fall through。
Ordinary absent/true/false/throw/nonboolean路径的semantic resolution/dispatch、gameplay、source/runtime、topology与notification均为
exact zero；kernel state与notification count保持identity/count不变。若callback显式触发replacement/suspend/dispose，测试只接受
该nested operation自身的exact delta，outer voice route不得再增加semantic/topology/notification mutation，且最终仍以
postcheck `stale`收口。

`.3b.1`的exact RED matrix为：

- **Raw/captured port**：`null`与exact one-key plain-object own-data callable可preflight；array/function、
  null/foreign prototype、extra string/symbol key、getter、inherited、missing、non-callable及prototype/ownKeys/descriptor
  throw在任何issuance前family-fault。Proxy证明只读structural descriptors、不触发value getter；caller method drift后仍只以
  original callable + exact receiver调用。
- **Type/API shape**：Admission新增且只新增`issueVoiceReplayAttemptInternalV1`；raw port、captured handle、zero-key
  attempt与四分支exact frozen result使用上述名称/shape，attempt复用existing direct-target proof且不新增ready-active-proof type，
  result只并入existing Narrative-local physical union，也不新增barrel/export/generic result。Borrowed issue返回`null`，borrowed
  route继续抛既有`ui.narrative_stable_action_admission_invalid`。
- **Kind/phase availability**：Ready-active Say在port present/absent及`confirm/auto`下均签发；choice/pause/custom与
  preparing/replacement/suspended/exiting/disposed返回`null`，barrier/empty construction仍unavailable。
- **Happy/closed outcomes**：Callback exact `true -> handled`、`false -> ignored`、absent `-> ignored`、
  throw/nonboolean `-> faulted`；absent路径证明zero callback/zero `sayCallbackClaim` install，其余路径证明shared claim exact CAS。
  全部Input consumed、surface routed、lower/semantic/topology/notify zero，attempt repeat stale且fresh attempt可再次执行。
- **Generic-before-spend**：Stale publication/gesture与unpublished/unmapped action保持consumer/callback zero且authentic
  attempt unspent；同一attempt随后通过fresh valid replay envelope只执行一次。
- **Attempt authenticity**：Spoof、clone、foreign admission、wrong kind、already-spent attempt均不能调用callback；
  cross-kind/unmapped probing不spend对应authentic attempt。
- **Pre-route lifecycle**：Target/source/frame replacement、retained preparing、suspension及bridge/admission/coordinator
  dispose在callback前closed；generic已截获时consumer为`null`且attempt unspent，否则authentic voice consumer返回family
  `stale`且attempt已spent；两者都callback/semantic zero。
- **Spend/reentry**：Callback内观察原attempt已spent；same-binding nested route被generic in-progress fence拒绝，nested voice
  issue为`null`；manual Say/content-auto issue/dispatch也因shared `sayCallbackClaim`不能进入。Caught reentry或ordinary callback
  结束后claim不poison，fresh attempt仍可执行。Claim conflict与post-claim currentness drift均为spent `stale`、zero callback并
  exact-CAS release；claim从不转移到semantic-in-flight slot。
- **Cross-binding hole**：Callback同步dispose old admission并在same target/source/frame建立successor admission；successor在
  outer shared claim存续时不能mint/token-route第二callback，`physicalActionAdmissionClaim` postcheck使outer为`stale`。Outer
  exact-CAS cleanup后successor可签发fresh attempt，old cleanup不能ABA-clear或poison successor。
- **Postcheck precedence**：Callback分别返回true/false、throw，同时nested替换target/source/frame、suspend或dispose；每项
  最终都必须是`stale`，且route只保留nested operation的exact delta，不追加semantic/topology/notify。

Implementation stop：若`.3b.1`需要直接调用raw opaque candidate identity、route时重读caller property、新增
`voiceReplayCallbackClaim`而不复用existing bridge `sayCallbackClaim`、把claim放在单个admission/binding、允许old cleanup无
identity CAS清理、为voice新增ready-active-proof type、在`null` handle路径安装callback claim、遗漏post-callback exact
`physicalActionAdmissionClaim`校验、把postcheck放在callback result分类之后、让port absent无法issue，或要求异步/Promise voice
callback，立即停止并修订owning design。若需要修改Dialogue definition/catalog/revision、generic Input/
Managed Surface action/result/receipt/code、public root或`./internal` barrel/package export，或需要semantic resolution、Coordinator/
topology mutation、persistent observer、React/Web/Host/live audio claimant、History replay、Auto/Skip mode/timer、Base interaction、
PlayerProfile、Save/Persistence、canonical/digest/replay/wire，也立即停止；这些都不属于`.3b.1`。

本amendment严格为docs-only：没有source、test、runtime、Host/React/Web/live claimant或delivery evidence，也不把此前
focused/UI/full/check/browser/examples/prebuilt结果冒充本批新验证。验证只要求本文件`deno fmt --check`与
`git diff --check`。该entry现只作为`.3b.1` delivery的historical contract保留；它当时把active current/next与
implementation gate推进到原 **S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.3c bridge-owned Auto/Skip transient mode floor**，该broad checkpoint现由
下述`.3c.0` amendment与`.3c.1` implementation取代。

### S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.3b.1 delivery — exact voice replay physical route

本delivery在既有source-relative Narrative family内实现`.3b.0`冻结的四个closed contract。
`NarrativeStableVoiceReplayPortInternalV1`只接受`null`或prototype exact为`Object.prototype`、own key exact only
`replayCurrentVoiceInternalV1`且该key为own data descriptor callable的plain object；malformed shape、symbol/extra key、accessor、
foreign/null prototype与prototype/ownKeys/descriptor trap都在candidate preflight归一化为
`narrative.candidate_preflight_faulted`，并保持source/occurrence/runtime/topology/notification allocation为零。Preflight只读
structural descriptor一次，将exact raw receiver与captured callable保存在package-private `WeakMap`，向admitted frame只交付frozen
zero-key `NarrativeStableCapturedVoiceReplayPortInternalV1` handle或`null`；route不重新读取raw property，因此caller后续替换method
不会改变已捕获callable或receiver。

既有`NarrativeStablePhysicalActionAdmissionInternalV1`只新增
`issueVoiceReplayAttemptInternalV1()`；它只为same-admission exact current ready-active Say签发frozen zero-key
`NarrativeStableVoiceReplayActionAttemptInternalV1`，复用现有
`ManagedSurfaceStableDirectActionTargetProofInternalV1`、stable input contract、direct target、source revision与exact admitted frame，
没有新增ready-active proof或第二binding/controller。Voice port absent仍可签发，使authenticated
`player.replay_voice`稳定得到family-local `ignored`。Result是exact frozen
`NarrativeStableVoiceReplayDispatchResultInternalV1`四分支`handled | ignored | stale | faulted`，只并入既有Narrative-local
`NarrativeStablePhysicalActionDispatchResultInternalV1`，不扩generic Surface/Input route、receipt或code。

Route先完整执行既有application/topology/input owner/routing lease/action catalog/input publication/gesture gate；generic gate未调用
consumer、unpublished或unmapped action均不spend authentic voice attempt。Exact `player.replay_voice` consumer先验证same-admission、
correct-kind、unspent opaque identity并立即spend，再按physical-admission claim、direct-target proof、current stable input
contract/target/source、exact Say frame及captured handle identity复验。Handle为`null`直接`ignored`且不读取、安装或释放callback claim；
non-null handle复用bridge-wide既有`sayCallbackClaim`与`saySemanticInFlightClaim` first-win fence，以exact token CAS覆盖callback
前后currentness复验，并只用`Reflect.apply(capturedCallable, exactReceiver, [])`同步调用一次。仍current时strict `true`为`handled`、
strict `false`为`ignored`、throw或non-boolean为`faulted`；callback内source/frame/suspension/dispose/successor drift先于return/throw分类，
统一以`stale`收口，finally只释放exact own claim。

Shared claim同时封闭same-binding route reentry、nested voice issuance、manual Say与content-auto issue/dispatch，也覆盖callback同步dispose
old admission并在same frame创建successor admission的cross-binding hole。Outer callback存续期间successor不能签发fresh voice/manual/
content-auto attempt，old attempt跨authority只能`stale`；outer postcheck以physical-admission claim drift返回`stale`，exact-CAS cleanup后
successor与先前未spend的content-auto token可正常继续，old cleanup不poison successor。四种voice family outcome的Input均保持
`consumed / input.managed_surface_consumed`，Surface保持`unchanged / surface.action_routed`，lower handler、semantic resolution、
Base dispatch与gameplay mutation为零；outer voice route不追加topology或notification mutation，callback显式触发的source/frame/
suspension/dispose只保留该nested operation自身的exact delta。

Mutation-sensitive matrix覆盖：raw null/exact/malformed port、transparent Proxy zero value-read、accessor getter zero invocation、captured
callable/receiver drift；exact API/zero-key handle/attempt/frozen result、borrowed receiver、clone/spoof/foreign/wrong-kind与cross-kind
attempt；present/absent及confirm/auto Say、non-Say/retained/suspended/terminal phase；true/false/throw/non-boolean/absent closed outcomes、
repeat/fresh attempt；generic-before-spend、pre-route source/frame/retained replacement与admission/coordinator terminal；shared-claim conflict、
same-binding in-progress、pre-signed content-auto dispatch、dispose/recreate successor及post-callback source/suspension/admission/bridge drift。

Runtime/test scope严格只有
`engine/packages/ui/src/narrative/narrative-managed-surface-family.ts`、同目录family test与
`engine/packages/ui/src/public-api.test.ts` negative guard。没有修改Dialogue definition/catalog/revision、
`playerInputActionIdsV1.replayVoice`或`player.replay_voice` spelling、generic action/Input合同、UI root、`./internal` barrel、package export、
