### S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.3c.0 entry amendment — exact bridge-owned Auto/Skip mode contract（已完成，docs-only）

原 **S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.3c bridge-owned Auto/Skip transient mode floor** 只保留为产品边界与历史
implementation pointer，不再直接实施；实际delivery线性拆为本`.3c.0` exact entry与`.3c.1`
implementation。Mode type精确命名为`NarrativeStablePlaybackModeInternalV1`，其closed value只有
`"normal" | "auto" | "skip"`。既有`NarrativeStablePublisherBridgeInternalV1`只新增read-only
`readPlaybackModeInternalV1(): NarrativeStablePlaybackModeInternalV1`；不得增加`setMode`、raw writer、public
store或第二controller authority。

Bridge record是唯一mode writer，并只strong-hold一个module-private、fresh frozen、own key exact only `mode`的current state
identity；fresh bridge先mint一个fresh normal identity，read method只投影current identity的primitive `mode`。每次effective Say
toggle都必须mint fresh successor state，即使value从`normal → auto → normal`也不得复用最初normal identity；mode transition不分配
numeric revision/counter，也不按source/frame建立strong history。Exact six-transition table固定为：

| Current state | Requested mode | Exact successor |
| ------------- | -------------- | --------------- |
| `normal`      | `auto`         | `auto`          |
| `auto`        | `auto`         | `normal`        |
| `skip`        | `auto`         | `auto`          |
| `normal`      | `skip`         | `skip`          |
| `auto`        | `skip`         | `skip`          |
| `skip`        | `skip`         | `normal`        |

One-shot physical attempt精确命名为`NarrativeStablePlaybackModeToggleActionAttemptInternalV1`，是frozen
zero-own-key branded capability。既有`NarrativeStablePhysicalActionAdmissionInternalV1`只新增
`issuePlaybackModeToggleAttemptInternalV1(requestedMode: "auto" | "skip"):
NarrativeStablePlaybackModeToggleActionAttemptInternalV1 | null`，并继续使用同一个bridge-owned
`physicalActionAdmissionClaim`、同一个contract-bound binding与既有`routeInternalV1`；不得新增mode binding、claimed
route、controller或gesture authority。Package-local `WeakMap` record只绑定exact issuing admission、direct-target proof、
direct target、source revision、admitted frame、requested mode、exact issuance mode-state identity与mutable one-shot `spent`；它不保存
semantic port、numeric revision、mode history、timer、observer或current-attempt strong slot。Attempt handle被caller释放后，其WeakMap
record与old issuance state可一并回收；bridge本身只strong-hold current state。

Family result精确命名为`NarrativeStablePlaybackModeToggleDispatchResultInternalV1`，其closed frozen rows为
`{ kind: "toggled", mode: NarrativeStablePlaybackModeInternalV1, completion: null } |
{ kind: "ignored", completion: null } | { kind: "stale", completion: null } |
{ kind: "faulted", completion: null }`。`toggled` own key exact为`kind | mode | completion`，其余三行own key exact为
`kind | completion`；三个successor mode各复用一个canonical toggled result identity，ignored/stale/faulted也各复用
canonical identity。该type只并入既有`NarrativeStablePhysicalActionDispatchResultInternalV1`的Narrative-local union，
不得扩张generic Managed Surface/Input result、receipt或code。

Issue availability固定如下：

- `requestedMode`只有exact `auto | skip`可进入capture；invalid runtime input、borrowed/retired admission、closed/non-current
  bridge、application/coordinator terminal或`bridgeRecord.sayCallbackClaim !== null`都返回`null`且zero attempt allocation。
- Exact current ready-active direct Dialogue `say | choice | pause | custom | presentation_barrier`均可签发。Say route按上述
  table toggle；choice、pause、custom与barrier只为published player control签发authentic attempt，使correct route得到consumed
  family-local `ignored`且mode保持normal。`.3c.1`因此可把barrier加入physical-admission construction kind whitelist，但该
  whitelist expansion只允许Auto/Skip mode control；不得签发Barrier semantic attempt、读取Stage proof或改变acknowledgment/run。
- Empty没有runtime/input owner且不构造physical admission；preparing、readiness gap、retained predecessor、suspended、exiting、
  publisher/bridge/admission/coordinator disposed也不可issue。Accepted non-Say boundary在ready前已经normal，若exact current
  non-Say route观察到其他mode identity则是family invariant fault，不得把它修复或分类为toggle。
- `sayCallbackClaim`只作为同步callback-stack reentry guard：claim存在时fresh issue为`null`，pre-signed authentic correct
  attempt若仍进入consumer则先spend再返回`stale`。Mode route从不安装、释放或转移该claim。它明确不读取、不占用也不清理
  `saySemanticInFlightClaim`；semantic Promise pending但callback stack已退出时仍可toggle future mode，且不得spend/retire
  existing manual activation或content-auto attempt。

Exact authenticated route precedence固定为：

1. 先完整通过既有application/terminal/coherence、topology/instance/input owner/routing lease/action catalog、input publication与
   gesture fences。Generic fence阻止consumer时`consumerResult`保持`null`，传入的authentic mode attempt不spend，Input/Surface
   receipt沿用既有合同。
2. Consumer只把exact `player.toggle_auto`映射到requested `auto`、`player.toggle_skip`映射到requested `skip`。Unmapped/
   unpublished action在读取attempt前返回既有结果；same-admission authentic mode token与另一toggle action或任意non-mode action
   mismatch也必须在spend前返回`unmapped`。该token随后仍可由其exact matching action使用；mode action也不得spend传入的其他
   authentic choice/pause/custom/Say/voice token。
3. Exact action/mode mapping再以WeakMap验证attempt为same-admission、correct mode-toggle kind且unspent。Spoof、clone、foreign
   admission、wrong kind与already-spent identity返回`stale`，且不得spend另一authentic capability。Same-admission correct-kind、
   requested-mode exact的attempt进入consumer后立即不可逆spend；后续ignored/stale/faulted均不回滚。Attempt record的exact issuance
   mode-state identity不是value/revision hint，而是后续final CAS必须仍命中的opaque predecessor。
4. Spend后依次复验`bridgeRecord.physicalActionAdmissionClaim === admissionClaim`、admission与bridge/application/coordinator active、
   `sayCallbackClaim === null`、direct-target proof、current stable input contract/direct target/source revision、exact admitted frame
   identity及current ready-active phase。Known lifecycle/proof/source/frame/state drift返回`stale`；captured authority fault或impossible
   mode/kind invariant返回`faulted`。这里不读取raw renderer、semantic port、clock、profile或mode subscriber。
5. Exact current Say先从attempt绑定的issuance state value按six-transition table计算successor mode，并在final proof/CAS前mint fresh
   frozen successor state、选择已存在的canonical toggled result；所有可能throw的allocation/freeze工作必须在final gate前完成，throw为
   spent `faulted`且mode不变。随后重验step 4全部currentness及
   `bridgeRecord.currentModeState === attempt.issuanceModeState`，winner才在无user callback/getter/allocation/notification的同步CAS
   commit point安装fresh successor并返回`toggled`。Exact current choice/pause/custom/barrier要求current/issuance state identity仍相同且
   mode已经是normal，返回`ignored`而不rotate identity。Mode commit后没有post-callback stale reclassification窗口；同一issuance
   state上pre-signed的distinct competitors只有first token可CAS成功，其余即使primitive mode后来ABA回到相同spelling也必须spent
   `stale`。Fresh attempt只有在前一commit后重新issue，才绑定fresh current state并可线性toggle。

`toggled | ignored | stale | faulted`四类family result只要consumer已进入，outer Input都为
`consumed / input.managed_surface_consumed`，Surface保持既有`unchanged / surface.action_routed`，lower context不fall through。
Mode route的semantic resolution、Base dispatch、gameplay、source/runtime、topology、identity high-water与composite/transient
notification全部为exact zero。`.3c.1`不得新增mode subscriber、listener set、notification queue、mode-specific revision或
observer；同步route result是toggle的唯一通知，S4.2 Host以后只消费该result及既有composite publication，不成为第二mode writer。
`readPlaybackModeInternalV1()`必须先以bridge/composite currentness fail closed：application/coordinator terminal、old application anchor或
closed bridge及currentness capture throw一律读取为`normal`，但该projection不伪造mode notification，也不允许issue/route复活old
state。Terminal在generic route前已经可见时consumer为`null`且attempt不spend；若terminal只在generic gate后、family consumer前的
reentrant callback发生，correct authentic attempt仍须先spend再以`stale`收口。两种precedence都保持zero mode mutation。

Mode lifecycle与stable reconcile必须共享同一bridge record并满足以下atomic order：

- Accepted Say→Say source/frame successor保留exact mode-state identity，包括new target preparing、readiness-failed gap、retry与
  fresh ready frame；old attempt仍因target/source/frame/admission drift失效。Temporary root suspension/resume也保留mode，旧attempt
  随direct ready-active/input proof撤销；`.3c.1`不保存reveal cursor、deadline或remaining。
- Accepted choice/pause/custom/barrier或greater-empty必须回到normal。若current mode非normal，reset须先mint fresh frozen normal
  successor；current已经normal时保持exact identity、不作无意义rotation。Reset只可在schema/canonical/preflight/
  admission plan全部成功后、existing composite apply与其captured listener notification之前pre-stage；因此既有state listener在
  source/runtime successor第一次可见时已经read到normal。若apply返回stale/faulted/rejected或在accepted install前throw，必须把
  exact predecessor mode identity原子restore，fresh failed successor变为unreachable，保持source/runtime/topology与两类notification既有
  zero-delta；schema/preflight
  rejection、same-occurrence byte-equal unchanged、same-occurrence canonical drift fault与issuance exhaustion也不得reset。
- Successful apply返回后不得再次写normal：existing composite listener可在完整successor上同步nested reconcile fresh Say并经fresh
  admission toggle mode；外层source reconcile不能clobber该later linearized mode。Listener throw只进既有diagnostics，不能rollback
  installed source或mode。Mode reset不新增第二notification；它只随该source commit既有的exact composite/transient notification
  vector可见。
- Physical admission dispose/recreate与Say reveal controller dispose/recreate不reset同一bridge的mode；它们只退休各自attempt/proof。
  Effective bridge dispose必须先关闭`bridgeActive`；current nonnormal时预先mint并安装fresh normal state，already-normal则不rotate，
  随后退休existing recovery generation并调用
  composite publisher-lease terminal dispose；terminal listener只能看到ingress closed + normal。Underlying fail-closed divergence不回滚
  bridge terminal mode，repeat dispose保持normal且不新增notification。Fresh application epoch/publisher lease只能经fresh bridge从normal
  开始；在bridge dispose前先发生的application/coordinator terminal也必须由read/currentness gate立即投影normal、拒绝issue/route，old
  token保持zero mode mutation。Old bridge/attempt不能把mode带入successor或在cleanup时ABA-write successor。

**S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.3c.1 acceptance：** implementation必须先交付以下mutation-sensitive RED matrix：

- **Type/state/result shape**：exact mode union、bridge own read method、fresh frozen one-key current/successor state、zero-key attempt、
  exact issuance-state binding、exact issue signature与四分支frozen result；六个transition逐行断言read value、fresh successor/old
  predecessor inequality、canonical result identity及exact own keys。Invalid requested mode与borrowed/retired issue为zero allocation；
  read-only API没有setter/subscriber。
- **Kind/phase availability**：Ready-active Say的`confirm | auto` policy均可toggle；ready-active choice/pause/custom/barrier均issue并
  consumed ignored；barrier route证明zero Stage/semantic/run mutation。Empty无admission，preparing/gap/retained/suspended/exiting/
  disposed不可issue，且现有non-mode action behavior与Dialogue definition catalog/revision保持exact。
- **Generic-before-spend与cross-kind**：stale application/topology/input publication/gesture、unpublished/unmapped action与wrong requested
  mode均不spend authentic attempt；同一token随后用fresh matching envelope只toggle一次。Spoof/clone/foreign/wrong-kind/repeat返回
  stale，且mode token与existing choice/pause/custom/Say/voice token互不烧掉。
- **Exact-state first-win/ABA**：同一current state预签auto/skip或同-mode多个attempt，first winning CAS安装fresh successor，其余均spent
  stale。保留最初normal attempt，另用fresh attempts完成`normal → auto → normal`后重放最初token，必须因old state identity而stale，
  不能按primitive `normal`误通过；failed CAS不安装已mint successor或改变canonical result identity。
- **Precommit currentness**：正确mapping先spend，再分别旋转target、source、frame、phase、physical admission、bridge/coordinator与
  direct input proof；known drift都stale/zero mode，authority/invariant fault为faulted，repeat保持stale。Success path证明state/result先
  detached-freeze、commit后无throw/rollback窗口。
- **Shared Say claims**：`sayCallbackClaim`已占用时fresh issue为`null`；pre-signed route为spent stale且不影响claim。Callback claim
  释放后fresh attempt正常。Manual/content-auto已持有`saySemanticInFlightClaim`的Promise-pending row仍可toggle，且claim token、manual/
  content-auto attempt spend/current slot与semantic dispatch count全部不变。
- **Source/lifecycle matrix**：active Auto/Skip分别经过Say→Say preparing/failed/retry/ready与root suspend/resume仍保留identity；经过
  choice/pause/custom/barrier/empty则fresh-reset normal并在existing listener首次调用前可见，already-normal boundary不rotate。Schema/
  preflight/admission/apply stale/fault路径恢复exact predecessor且zero notification；listener throw不rollback，listener nested fresh Say +
  toggle不被outer reset覆盖。
- **Owner successor/terminal**：physical admission与reveal controller dispose/recreate保留mode；bridge dispose listener读到inactive +
  normal，underlying fault与repeat dispose不复活。Raw application/coordinator terminal在bridge disposal前也使read fail-closed normal、
  issue unavailable；terminal-before-generic保持consumer `null`/attempt unspent，generic-after-terminal race进入family consumer则spent
  stale，两者route都zero mode。Fresh bridge/epoch/lease从fresh normal开始，old attempt/cleanup不能跨authority写mode。
- **Zero delta/public boundary**：每个physical result都证明lower/semantic/source/runtime/topology/composite/transient notification zero；
  reset只复用existing source/terminal notification，不出现mode observer。Mode/attempt/result/read method只有source-relative type/runtime
  import；UI root、`./internal` barrel与package export均以negative tests保持缺席。
- **Boundedness**：10,000次toggle、Say/non-Say source rotation、suspend/resume与admission successor只由bridge strong-hold当前一个
  fresh state；没有listener/queue/numeric revision/history/tombstone或attempt strong slot，canonical result identity有界。Old state只可被
  caller仍持有的attempt经WeakMap record间接保留，caller释放attempt后provenance与old state均可回收。

Implementation stop：若`.3c.1`需要public/generic mode store、setter/subscriber、第二mode writer/controller/binding/claimed route、
mode notification或把mode塞入generic composite/public Surface state，立即停止并修订owning design。若non-Say/empty reset无法在existing
composite notification前pre-stage且在failed apply上restore，或outer source reconcile必须在listener返回后无条件重写normal，也立即停止。
若需要读取/占用/清理`saySemanticInFlightClaim`、改变`sayCallbackClaim` ownership、retire/spend manual或content-auto attempt、
实现timer/clock/deadline/remaining/reveal cursor、`autoWaitMs`/skip scheduling、automatic semantic dispatch、same-transition semantic
first-win、History topology、React/Web/Host/live claimant或legacy `PlaybackControllerV1` dual write，全部停止并留S4.2/S4.3。若需要修改
Dialogue action catalog/revision、generic Input/Managed Surface result/receipt/code、UI root或`./internal` barrel/package export，或需要

### S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.3c.1 delivery — bridge-owned Auto/Skip transient mode（已完成）

Narrative publisher bridge现在是player playback mode的唯一writer：新增exact
`NarrativeStablePlaybackModeInternalV1 = "normal" | "auto" | "skip"`与read-only
`readPlaybackModeInternalV1()`，bridge record只strong-hold一个fresh frozen、own-key exact one `mode`的
current state。Fresh bridge从fresh normal identity开始；每个effective Say toggle都先mint detached fresh successor state，
再以exact issuance-state CAS提交，因此`normal → auto → normal`不会复用最初normal identity，pre-signed competitor与scalar
ABA replay均spent `stale`。三个mode spelling各有canonical frozen `toggled` result，`ignored | stale | faulted`也各复用
bounded canonical identity。

既有physical admission只新增
`issuePlaybackModeToggleAttemptInternalV1(requestedMode: "auto" | "skip")`。Fresh frozen zero-key attempt的package-local
WeakMap provenance绑定exact admission、requested mode、direct-target proof、target/source/frame与issuance mode-state identity，
不建立第二binding、claimed route、controller、revision、subscriber或current-attempt strong slot。Exact
`player.toggle_auto | player.toggle_skip`按six-transition table切换current Say；wrong requested mode与non-mode action mismatch在
spend前返回既有`unmapped`，spoof/clone/foreign/wrong-kind/repeat fail closed，其他choice/pause/custom/Say/voice authentic
capability也不会被mode probe烧掉。Ready-active choice/pause/custom与presentation Barrier可签发mode attempt，但correct route只
consumed family-local `ignored`并保持normal；Barrier不签发其他physical capability，也不读取或改变Stage、run、acknowledgment或
semantic authority。所有mode physical outcome保持existing Input consumed/Surface unchanged，semantic、gameplay、source/runtime、
topology与notification exact zero。

Mode reset与stable source commit共用同一个bridge record：current mode非normal时，accepted
choice/pause/custom/barrier或greater-empty只在schema、canonical、preflight及admission plan成功后、existing composite
apply/notification前pre-stage fresh normal；already-normal boundary保留exact mode-state identity。non-applied或pre-install throw以
exact CAS恢复predecessor，successful apply返回后不再post-write，因此listener内安装的fresh Say successor/toggle不会被outer reset
覆盖。Accepted Say→Say preparing/readiness-failed/retry/ready与temporary root suspension/resume保留exact mode identity，但旧
target/source/frame/input attempt仍stale；physical admission或Say reveal controller dispose/recreate也不reset mode。Effective bridge
dispose先关闭ingress并让terminal listener只读到normal；application/coordinator terminal同样通过currentness projection fail closed，
terminal-before-generic保持consumer `null`，generic gate后发生的terminal race则先spend再返回canonical `stale`。
`sayCallbackClaim`仍只是同步callback-stack guard：claim存续时fresh issue为`null`、pre-signed route为spent stale；mode path不读取、
占用或清理`saySemanticInFlightClaim`，所以manual semantic Promise pending但callback stack已退出时仍可toggle future mode。
10,000次toggle、source/readiness/suspension/admission successor churn证明bridge只strong-hold当前state，旧state只由caller仍持有的
WeakMap-keyed attempt间接保留。

本delivery仍是source-relative、package-internal dormant floor；UI root与`./internal` barrel/package export以negative tests保持
缺席，也没有generic mode store/result、setter/subscriber、mode notification、Base/PlayerProfile/Save/Persistence或

### S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.3d.0 exact entry amendment — History exact-parent open intent（已完成，docs-only）

原`.3d` broad checkpoint只作产品语义与历史implementation pointer保留，不再直接实施；实际runtime delivery线性拆为
本`.3d.0` exact entry与`.3d.1` implementation。本entry只冻结source-relative contract、RED与stop，不交付
source、test、runtime、Host或live capability。

Candidate的History入口可用性与后续内容观察是两个独立read-only seam。本切片新增exact raw
`NarrativeStableHistoryAvailabilityPortInternalV1`：它只允许prototype exact为`Object.prototype`、非array、
own key exact只有`readHistoryAvailabilityInternalV1`的plain object；该key必须是own data descriptor，所以exact member为
`readonly readHistoryAvailabilityInternalV1: () => boolean`。Preflight只读descriptor一次，把exact receiver与callable存入
package-local `WeakMap`，只向`NarrativeStableCandidateSnapshotInternalV1.historyAvailabilityPort`写入fresh frozen
zero-own-key branded `NarrativeStableCapturedHistoryAvailabilityPortInternalV1` handle。Route只可以
`Reflect.apply(capturedCallable, exactReceiver, [])`调用，不得重读raw object；后续替换、删除或改成accessor不改变
captured binding。Malformed object、inherited/accessor/noncallable member、extra string/symbol key、foreign prototype或reflection trap均在
identity/source/runtime/topology mutation前收敛为既有`narrative.candidate_preflight_faulted`。Candidate required-port closed set新增
`narrative.history_availability`；missing port仍经既有`narrative.required_port_missing`返回该exact `portId`。

现有opaque `historyObservationPort`字段及`narrative.history_observation` required-port ID保持exact不变；本切片不读、解析或
收窄它，也不把availability boolean写入该opaque identity。它继续只作S4.2 History content observation的reserved seam；
`.3d.1`不冻结History entries、content revision、subscriber、renderer props或child parameter。这一分离避免用one-key boolean port
提前封死S4.2的content contract，也不建立第二History content authority。

Physical capability精确命名为frozen zero-own-key branded
`NarrativeStableHistoryOpenActionAttemptInternalV1`。既有
`NarrativeStablePhysicalActionAdmissionInternalV1`只新增
`issueHistoryOpenAttemptInternalV1(): NarrativeStableHistoryOpenActionAttemptInternalV1 | null`，并继续复用同一
bridge-owned `physicalActionAdmissionClaim`、contract-bound binding与`routeInternalV1`；不新增History controller、
binding、claimed route或generic input envelope。Attempt的package-local `WeakMap` record exact绑定
`kind: "history_open"`、exact issuing admission、existing stable action authority、
`ManagedSurfaceStableDirectActionTargetProofInternalV1`、direct target、source revision、exact admitted frame、
captured availability handle与mutable `spent`。Issue不读availability callback；它只在same-admission、bridge/application/coordinator
active、stable input contract current、direct target ready-active，且frame kind为`say | choice | pause | custom |
presentation_barrier`之一时mint。Empty、preparing、readiness gap/failed、retained predecessor、suspended、terminal、
missing/foreign captured handle都返回`null`。

Open capability精确命名为frozen zero-own-key branded
`NarrativeStableHistoryOpenIntentInternalV1`。Family result精确命名为
`NarrativeStableHistoryOpenDispatchResultInternalV1`，且只有四个exact frozen row：

- `{ kind: "requested", intent: NarrativeStableHistoryOpenIntentInternalV1, completion: null }`，own key exact为
  `kind | intent | completion`；
- `{ kind: "ignored", completion: null } | { kind: "stale", completion: null } |
  { kind: "faulted", completion: null }`，每行own key exact为`kind | completion`。

该result只作为`NarrativeStablePhysicalActionDispatchResultInternalV1`的Narrative-local union member进入既有route
`consumerResult`；不修改generic `ManagedSurfaceAuthenticatedActionRouteResultInternalV1`、Input result/receipt、
action binding或result code。四类family outcome都要求outer route的Input为
`consumed / input.managed_surface_consumed`、Surface unchanged，lower fallthrough为zero。Input consumed是已认证catalog route的属性，
不是第五个family result kind。

Ordered precedence冻结如下：application/terminal/global coherence、topology/instance/input owner/routing lease、action catalog、
input publication与gesture先由既有authenticated route完整验证；consumer未调用时`consumerResult` exact为`null`，
supplied attempt不spent。Consumer只把`player.toggle_history`映射为`history_open`；unmapped action在读取attempt前返回
既有`unmapped`。Null/spoof/clone、foreign admission/bridge、wrong-kind或repeat返回family-local `stale`，不能spend
另一个authentic capability；authentic History attempt用于non-History action或其他authentic attempt用于History action时均在
spend前按既有mismatch/unmapped policy收口，原token仍可在correct route使用。只有same-admission、correct-kind、
unspent exact identity进入consumer后立即one-shot spend，之后任何outcome都不退回attempt。

Spend后依次复验physical admission claim/bridge lifecycle、existing `sayCallbackClaim === null`、
`saySemanticInFlightClaim === null`、direct-target proof、current stable input contract、exact target/source/frame/kind与
captured availability-handle identity。Known proof/lifecycle drift返回`stale`；captured authority/invariant fault返回`faulted`。
History只读`saySemanticInFlightClaim`；它绝不安装、转移、清理或延长semantic claim。Semantic Promise已first-win时，
History是面向当前parent的即时topology request，因此fresh issue为`null`；pre-signed correct attempt进入consumer后先spend，
再返回`stale`且不mint intent。这与mode只设置future policy、故不参与semantic-inflight的边界不同。

Availability invocation复用bridge既有`sayCallbackClaim`作为唯一同步callback-stack fence，不新增
`historyAvailabilityCallbackClaim`或平行slot。Winner用fresh frozen zero-key token exact-CAS安装该claim，再复验上述
admission/proof/frame/handle及semantic-inflight仍为`null`，然后以zero arguments调用captured callable。Callback返回后在
outer仍exact-own claim时先复验全部currentness，所以dispose/successor、parent replacement/preparation、source/frame
retry、suspension、empty、bridge/application/coordinator terminal优先于callback throw/return收敛为`stale`。仍current时，
exact `false`返回`ignored`，exact `true`才mint fresh intent并返回`requested`；throw或任意non-boolean返回
`faulted`。Finally只以exact-token CAS释放`sayCallbackClaim`，foreign/old cleanup不得清除successor token。

Same-binding callback route reentry仍先被generic `ui.managed_surface_action_route_in_progress`拒绝，且nested attempt不spent。
Callback内dispose old admission并recreate same-bridge binding也不能绕过：existing claim存续时fresh History/Say/manual/
content-auto/voice/mode issue按各自已冻结guard返回`null`，pre-signed correct History route先spend再`stale`。`.3d.1`
不为choice/pause/custom/barrier改造既有claim检查；这些已认证nested operation仍由它们的existing authority产生exact
delta，而outer History在callback后因parent/source/frame drift返回`stale`且不追加任何mutation。全operation的
same-composition transition serialization仍属S4.2，本切片不用新global claim提前实现它。

Exact `true`只在post-callback currentness全部通过后mint intent。Intent的package-local `WeakMap` provenance exact绑定
Narrative bridge/application lifetime、existing stable action authority、route已认证的
`ManagedSurfaceStableDirectActionTargetProofInternalV1`、direct target、source revision、exact admitted frame与mutable `spent`；
availability handle只属于route-time attempt/currentness，不重复进入future intent provenance。
不存入raw Coordinator、parent handle/instance ID、gesture/envelope或caller可复制的target spelling。该intent是authenticated route向
S4.2 lifecycle authority的handoff，因此不再绑定physical admission lifetime；route已返回后只dispose/recreate admission而
exact parent proof/source/frame仍current，不会单独使intent stale。Parent replacement preparation/cutover、same-target source/readiness retry、
root suspension（即使后续resume）、empty、bridge/application/coordinator terminal都使该exact proof不可复活，S4.2只能使用fresh
intent。Availability的exact `true`是route-time已验证授权；S4.2不重调callback，但必须重验exact-parent currentness。

`.3d.1`只交付intent mint，明确不新增`consume`/`redeem`/`isCurrent` API，不在topology transition之前单独spend
intent。因此它只能把intent表述为**future one-use capability**，不宣称本切片已执行或验证redemption one-shot。
S4.2必须在same-composition transition fence内把intent provenance/currentness复验、one-way spend与Coordinator exact-parent
History child preparation/open组成一个atomic operation；任一non-applied/fault的exact spend/rollback policy届时一并冻结和测试，不允许
`.3d.1`用无topology的提前consume制造TOCTOU。

`.3d.1` production/test source scope exact只允许三个文件：

- `engine/packages/ui/src/narrative/narrative-managed-surface-family.ts`；
- `engine/packages/ui/src/narrative/narrative-managed-surface-family.test.ts`；
- `engine/packages/ui/src/public-api.test.ts`。

原Dialogue definition revision `2`与`player.toggle_history` catalog、generic input ID、legacy `DialoguePanelV1`/
`VnLayerV1`、Engine Lab/Story writer、Base/Web/React Host及live graph均必须zero diff。上述raw/captured port、attempt、
intent、result与issue method只允许从Narrative source file直接import；UI root、`./internal` barrel与package export必须以
type/runtime negative tests保持不可达。Bridge/admission不强持attempt或intent；两者provenance都只以caller-held opaque
WeakMap key可达，canonical `ignored | stale | faulted`保持bounded identity，10,000次issue/route/callback/source/
admission churn后production strong state仍为O(1)。

`.3d.1` mutation-sensitive RED matrix冻结如下：

1. **Port/preflight exactness**：raw/captured type、snapshot field、new required-port ID、one-key own-data descriptor、zero-key handle与
   receiver/callable capture；覆盖accessor/inherited/extra string+symbol/array/function/foreign prototype/noncallable/reflection trap，以及
   capture后raw member replace/delete/accessor mutation仍调用original binding。同时证明opaque `historyObservationPort`的identity/type未被读取或收窄。
2. **Attempt/result shape**：exact type/method spelling、borrowed issue `null`、borrowed route仍抛既有invalid error；attempt/intent
   frozen zero-key，`requested`与其他三行exact own keys/completion，每次successful request的intent identity fresh。全部新name在
   UI root、`./internal`与package export有type/runtime negative guard。
3. **Kinds/phases**：exact ready-active `say | choice | pause | custom | presentation_barrier`都可issue并在exact `true`下
   requested；exact `false`为ignored，throw/nonboolean为faulted。Empty/preparing/readiness-failed gap/retained/suspended/terminal无issue，
   且availability callback count、lower/semantic/gameplay/source/runtime/topology/notification delta精确可观察。
4. **Mapping/spend precedence**：generic application/topology/lease/catalog/publication/gesture failure与consumer-not-called均不spend；
   unmapped/wrong-action/wrong-kind probe不烧掉authentic token；spoof/clone/foreign/repeat fail closed。Correct attempt一进consumer即spend，
   之后`requested | ignored | stale | faulted`都是one-shot，repeat stale/zero callback。
5. **Callback claims/reentry**：existing `sayCallbackClaim`已占用时issue `null`，pre-signed correct route spent-stale；
   availability callback内same-binding route走generic in-progress且token unspent，dispose/recreate cross-binding不得nested History/Say/
   content-auto/voice/mode callback或mint第二intent。Finally exact-CAS释放own claim，throw、nonboolean、stale、dispose全分支都覆盖，
   old cleanup不能ABA-clear successor claim。
6. **Semantic first-win boundary**：`saySemanticInFlightClaim`非`null`时fresh issue `null`，pre-signed route spent-stale、
   callback/intent/topology exact zero；History不install/release/transfer该claim。Choice/pause/custom/barrier nested authorized operation不新增
   claim guard；它们若改变parent/source/frame，outer postcheck稳定stale并只证明outer-added delta为zero。
7. **Post-callback currentness**：callback内分别做target replacement/preparation、source/readiness retry、frame/handle drift、root
   suspension、physical admission dispose/recreate、bridge/application/coordinator terminal，证明currentness drift优先于true/false/throw收敛
   `stale`，不留intent、claim、topology或notification。纯admission successor如果已使outer admission claim失效，当次outer仍stale；
   只有route完整返回后已mint intent的future lifetime才与admission解耦。
8. **Intent floor/boundedness**：10,000次true/false/throw、source/frame、suspension与admission successor churn不在bridge留
   current/retired intent slot、array、revision、tombstone、subscriber或callback history；caller保留的requested intent仍fresh/frozen/opaque。
   S4.2的future RED必须另证intent exact-parent invalidation、admission-independence、one-use spend与Coordinator child-open同atomicity；
   `.3d.1`不用test-only redemption hook伪造该证据。

Implementation stop：若`.3d.1`需要把availability并入/收窄opaque `historyObservationPort`、读History content或提前冻结
S4.2 child props/subscriber，立即停止并修订owning design。若需要新controller/binding/claimed route/callback slot、修改既有
choice/pause/custom/barrier claim policy、与`saySemanticInFlightClaim`共享ownership，或无法以post-callback exact recheck让nested
operation first-win，也停止。若intent mint必须调用Coordinator、暴露raw parent/target identity、分配History occurrence/instance、
修改topology/readiness/input/focus/dismiss/root suspension/timer，或新增standalone consume/redeem/currentness API，这些都越界到S4.2。
S4.2若无法把intent validation/spend与Coordinator child preparation组成同atomic transition，也必须在S4.2 RED前停止，
不能回到`.3d.1`补一个拆分spend API。若需要修改Dialogue catalog/revision、generic Input/Managed Surface result/
receipt/code、UI root/`./internal` barrel/package export、React/Web/live claimant、legacy writer，或Base、PlayerProfile、
Save/Persistence/canonical/digest/replay/wire，均不属于`.3d.1`。

### S4.1b.1b.1b.2b.3d.1 delivery — History exact-parent open intent mint（已完成）

本delivery实现availability与content observation的单一职责分离。Candidate继续原样保留既有opaque
`historyObservationPort`及required ID `narrative.history_observation`，不读取、收窄或复制History内容；另新增exact plain-object raw
`NarrativeStableHistoryAvailabilityPortInternalV1`，其sole own-data callable为
`readHistoryAvailabilityInternalV1(): boolean`。Preflight只捕获一次exact receiver/callable并把fresh frozen zero-own-key
`NarrativeStableCapturedHistoryAvailabilityPortInternalV1`写入candidate snapshot；malformed/trapping present snapshot继续走既有
preflight fault，closed required-port set新增`narrative.history_availability`，缺失时仍以
`narrative.required_port_missing`携带该exact `portId`。

既有`NarrativeStablePhysicalActionAdmissionInternalV1`只新增
`issueHistoryOpenAttemptInternalV1()`，既有`routeInternalV1`只把`player.toggle_history`映射为`history_open`。
`NarrativeStableHistoryOpenActionAttemptInternalV1`与`NarrativeStableHistoryOpenIntentInternalV1`都是frozen zero-own-key opaque
identity；`NarrativeStableHistoryOpenDispatchResultInternalV1`只有fresh
`{ kind: "requested", intent, completion: null }`与canonical
`{ kind: "ignored" | "stale" | "faulted", completion: null }`四行exact shape，并且只扩张Narrative-local physical result union，
不改变generic Managed Surface/Input result或receipt；四类family outcome的outer Input都保持consumed，Surface unchanged且lower
fallthrough为zero。Attempt的package-local `WeakMap` provenance绑定issuing admission、existing
stable action authority、direct-target proof、exact direct parent、source revision、admitted frame、captured availability handle与mutable
`spent`；minted intent只绑定bridge、stable action authority、同一proof/parent/source/frame与mutable `spent`，不携带availability handle、
physical admission、raw Coordinator、parent ID、gesture或可复制target spelling。

Exact current ready-active `say | choice | pause | custom | presentation_barrier`五种frame都可签发attempt；empty、preparing、gap、
retained、suspended或terminal均无capability。Captured availability以exact receiver、exact callable、zero arguments同步调用一次：exact
`false`为`ignored`，exact `true`才mint fresh intent并返回`requested`，throw或non-boolean为`faulted`。Presentation Barrier的true路径同样
只mint intent，Stage acknowledgment、semantic dispatch、topology与notification delta全部为zero。

Route precedence保持generic fence优先：application/terminal、topology/instance/input owner、routing lease、catalog、publication与gesture任一
失败都不会进入consumer或spend supplied token。Unmapped action与authentic cross-kind probe在spend前收口，null/spoof/foreign/repeat fail
closed；只有same-admission correct-kind unspent History attempt进入consumer后立即one-shot spend，再执行family currentness分类。
Availability调用复用existing bridge `sayCallbackClaim`，以fresh token安装并在`finally`仅用exact-token CAS释放；issue、pre-call与post-call都要求
existing `saySemanticInFlightClaim === null`，History不安装、清理或转移semantic claim。Same-binding callback reentry继续由generic
route-in-progress拒绝，cross-binding History/Say/content-auto/voice/mode也受既有claim fence；choice/pause/custom/barrier不被本批改写，
其authorized nested operation拥有自己的exact delta，outer History只在callback后因parent/source/frame drift返回`stale`且不追加mutation。
Callback后的admission、bridge、direct parent、proof、input contract、source、frame、availability handle、callback/semantic claim复验先于
throw/boolean分类，因此dispose/successor、replacement/preparation、retry、suspension、empty或terminal drift都稳定收敛为spent
`stale`；old cleanup不能ABA-clear successor claim。

本切片只交付mint-time freshness与provenance，并把intent作为交给S4.2的future one-use capability；没有新增
`consume`、`redeem`或`isCurrent` API，也不以test-only hook声称已验证post-mint admission independence、lifecycle invalidation或one-shot
redemption。S4.2必须在same-composition transition fence内原子复验并spend intent，同时执行Coordinator exact-parent History child
prepare/open；History close、focus/dismiss、root suspension与timer coordination也继续留在S4.2。

Runtime/test scope严格只有family source、同目录family test与UI public-API negative guard。所有新raw/captured/attempt/intent/result type及

Host、live Story writer、History content、Coordinator/topology、input/focus/dismiss、timer、persistence或wire扩张。Bridge/admission不新增
attempt/intent strong slot、array、revision、tombstone、subscriber或callback history；opaque keys只由caller retention决定生命周期，canonical
non-requested results保持bounded identity，10,000次issue/route/churn证明production strong state保持O(1)。

#### Cross-axis ownership and exact package-internal seams

Live checkout中的Dialogue ready instance属于stable runtime，**不在**
`ManagedSurfaceReducerStateV1.publication.orderedInstances`；generic
`ManagedSurfaceCoordinatorV1.pushTransientChild()`与transient reducer `prepare_child`只会在该transient vector查找parent，因而不能也
不得把stable Dialogue伪造成generic transient parent。History仍按owning design保持Coordinator/composite-owned **transient**
child，不得改成stable published target、第二stable publisher、React-local boolean或generic Coordinator compatibility wrapper。

S4.2.1在`managed-surface-reducer.ts`新增唯一source-relative pure helper
`deriveManagedSurfaceReducerCrossAxisChildPreparationInternalV1()`。它只接受stable-composite authority已经认证并投影的external
parent instance/definition、module-owned child definition与fresh candidate；ordinary `reduceManagedSurfaceV1({ kind:
"prepare_child" })`及generic Coordinator仍只接受transient-vector parent。Pure helper只构造transient History
`preparing / child_open` candidate，并把其`parentInstanceId`设为exact authenticated stable runtime instance ID；它不检查或持有
stable proof，不可由Story、renderer、Host或public caller直接形成cross-axis authority。

`managed-surface-stable-composite-state.ts`新增唯一source-relative
`ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildAuthorityInternalV1`及
`claimManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildAuthorityInternalV1(kernel, exactClaimant)`。Narrative family module以
package-private `WeakMap`为每个exact composite kernel只mint一个fresh frozen zero-own-key **composition claimant**，立即claim并随
kernel lifetime retained；同一kernel上的predecessor bridge dispose与successor bridge construction复用same claimant/returned
authority，不mint或transfer第二个claimant。每个exact composite kernel只有first exact claimant能取得mutation authority，same
claimant repeat claim复用同一retained authority，foreign claimant、foreign kernel、clone或borrowed
claim在读取definition/proof/guard前抛`ui.managed_surface_stable_exact_parent_transient_child_claim_invalid`并保持state/identity/
notification及caller getter exact zero。Composition claimant没有export、factory或caller-supplied mint path；其他module不能取得它，
也没有release/reclaim API。Application/kernel successor使用fresh claimant；same-kernel Narrative bridge successor只复用family-private
composition authority。其唯一mutation method
`prepareExactParentTransientChildInternalV1(input)`的input own-data fields exact为
`parentProof | expectedParent | expectedSourceRevision | definition | semanticOccurrenceId | commitGuard`。该method只接受本kernel的
ready-active `ManagedSurfaceStableDirectActionTargetProofInternalV1`、同record的exact admitted parent/source、resolved module-owned
History definition、`semanticOccurrenceId: null`与Narrative-internal commit guard。它不接受raw parent handle/instance ID、generic action
envelope、caller-supplied candidate、async/Promise gate或Story callback。

`commitGuard`精确为frozen one-method
`ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildCommitGuardInternalV1`；sole own string key是own-data callable
`commitInternalV1(candidate: ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildCandidateInternalV1): boolean`，没有
symbol/extra/inherited/accessor key。Authority在任何transition前descriptor-capture exact guard
receiver/callable；完整successor已pure-plan、prevalidate且existing prepared-install commit已capture notification vector后，才把该captured
callable作为assignment前immediate no-throw gate调用一次，并传入本次exact candidate。

Authority内部结果精确命名为`ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildPreparationResultInternalV1`。Fresh frozen zero-own-key
`ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildCandidateInternalV1`只作为composite→Narrative handoff；result只有exact frozen
`{ kind: "installed", candidate } | { kind: "stale" } | { kind: "faulted" }`，own key分别exact为
`kind | candidate`、`kind`、`kind`。Candidate private record持有已安装的exact child published instance、readiness authority及cross-axis
handoff provenance；它不成为topology authority/store，也没有raw field、method、close/readiness API或public identity。Narrative把
`installed` canonical map为下述family `preparing`；
generic result不进入Coordinator/result/receipt/code，raw kernel/claimant/proof/parent/instance/readiness也不返回Narrative caller。

Composite authority先在同一current state中认证proof record的authority/kernel/application epoch/topology revision/runtime instance/
direct parent/source，要求该stable instance仍是exact ready-active、non-retained direct target；再检查owner、child-only slot、layer、
`single` cardinality与无existing exact-parent History child，fresh分配candidate identity并通过上述pure reducer helper及existing
whole-composite reflow构造完整successor。Cross-axis authenticity只从composite-authorized state lineage、shared-cursor child instance的
exact identity/`parentInstanceId`及current stable runtime attempt/slot/owner/layer做structural derivation；generic transient evidence、可复制
ID或相同spelling不能重建。Candidate WeakMap record只作Narrative handoff/readiness evidence，不是persistent second topology store；若
implementation证明现有结构不足以完成exact validation，必须stop/amend，不能静默新增cross-axis collection。

Whole-composite topology/coherence从此把该authenticated cross-axis link视为唯一parent edge：History preparation的blocking fallback
在同一个successor中使exact Dialogue root suspended；History ready/failure与close留给后续slice，但其structural effect只能沿这条edge。
Stable root replacement preparation必须retain exact root + History subtree，replacement failure继续retain；ready cutover、greater-empty、
publisher/bridge disposal、application successor或Coordinator terminal必须在同一个install中retire root + History subtree。Orphan、
foreign-origin、parent
attempt/target/source mismatch、duplicate slot或不能从current composite lineage structurally derive的dangling `parentInstanceId`均使
composite validation fail closed，不得静默把History提升为root、转挂到successor parent或留在transient publication。Generic
Coordinator始终不能spoof、open、reparent或adopt该cross-axis child。

Narrative侧新增frozen `NarrativeStableHistoryChildLifecycleInternalV1`，由
`createNarrativeStableHistoryChildLifecycleInternalV1({ bridge })`为exact bridge创建；factory只从bridge-private WeakMap record取得exact
composite runtime kernel及上述composition claimant/authority，不接受redundant caller kernel。每个Narrative lifecycle仍exact绑定一个
active bridge：predecessor bridge dispose使old
lifecycle/intent permanently stale；same-kernel successor创建fresh lifecycle并复用underlying composition claimant，而不能借old bridge
的frame/proof/intent。Foreign Narrative lifecycle不能借另一个bridge的composite mutation seam。其唯一method
`redeemHistoryOpenIntentInternalV1(intent)`只接受`.3d.1` minted intent。成功返回的
`NarrativeStableHistoryChildPreparationInternalV1`是fresh frozen zero-own-key opaque identity；package-local `WeakMap` record绑定
exact bridge/composite authority、direct parent/source/frame、child preparation identity及future readiness evidence，不暴露raw
occurrence/instance/parent ID。S4.2.1唯一family result
`NarrativeStableHistoryChildPreparationResultInternalV1`只有三个exact frozen row：

- `{ kind: "preparing", preparation: NarrativeStableHistoryChildPreparationInternalV1, completion: null }`，own key exact为
  `kind | preparation | completion`；
- `{ kind: "stale", completion: null } | { kind: "faulted", completion: null }`，own key exact为`kind | completion`。

`stale`与`faulted`各为canonical bounded identity；每次applied preparation的preparation/result均fresh。没有`ignored`、`closed`、
`ready`、generic receipt/code或semantic completion row；preparation本身没有method，availability已在`.3d.1` route-time完成，`.1`不重调
callback。上述authority、
preparation/result与两层cross-axis helper只允许source-relative direct import，UI root、`./internal` barrel与package exports必须保持
type/runtime negative guard。

#### Atomic plan, spend, install and rollback protocol

Redemption entry先验证borrowed `this`、bridge/application/Coordinator active及intent是该bridge的exact package-local record。Null、
clone、spoof、foreign bridge/kernel或already-spent intent直接返回canonical `stale`，不触碰composite planner。Physical admission
不在intent provenance中；route完整返回后只dispose/recreate admission且parent proof/source/frame仍exact，不影响redemption。

Authentic unspent intent随后要求record中的stable action authority、direct-parent proof、parent/source/frame与bridge current projection
exact相同，且`sayCallbackClaim === null`、`saySemanticInFlightClaim === null`。Parent replacement preparation/cutover、source/readiness
retry、frame drift、root suspension（即使随后resume）、empty或bridge/application/Coordinator terminal均返回`stale`；intent保持
unspent但其exact proof不可复活。Another History child/preparation会先改变topology/parent phase，因此旧intent同样stale，而不是
`already_open`新row。两个authentic intent竞争同一vacancy时只允许first winner spend/install；loser保持unspent但其
topology-bound proof永久stale，winner child后来close、root恢复ready-active也不能复活该loser。Fresh vacancy必须重新mint fresh intent。

只有上述currentness通过才调用cross-axis composite authority pure-plan完整successor。Unknown proof/coherence/capacity/
cross-axis invariant或reducer/reflow fault返回`faulted`；state、identity high-water、topology/publication、notification、candidate
record与intent `spent`全部exact zero。该authentic intent保持unspent，并且只在同一proof仍exact时允许retry；一旦其他transition推进，
普通stale规则优先。Known parent/proof/slot drift与prepared install stale/abort返回`stale`，同样不spend、不install。

Successful plan必须先通过existing runtime kernel `prepareStateInstallInternalV1(expectedState, nextState)`完成lineage、installability与
composite invariant validation，再把preflight阶段已经descriptor-captured的exact commit-guard receiver/callable、candidate与argument
vector收窄成existing zero-argument gate，交给`commitPreparedStateInstallInternalV1(prepared, gate)`提交。Narrative gate不是Story/caller
callback；它只在kernel
`transitionInProgress` fence内、state assignment前执行一次no-throw exact CAS：复验same intent record仍`spent === false`、same
bridge/authority/parent/source/frame及callback/semantic claims，只原子写入`spent = true`并返回exact `true`。Gate不能注册其他state、
调用Coordinator、读取raw port、分配identity、通知listener或dispatch semantic。Generic install返回`installed + candidate`后，Narrative
才以already-frozen opaque preparation建立private handoff record并返回family `preparing`；该mapping不重入、不通知也不成为第二topology
commit。

Gate未调用、返回false、prepared token stale/invalid或commit abort时不得spend/install；gate throw/non-boolean属于implementation fault且
不得成为accepted Narrative gate。只有prepared successor实际installed才返回`preparing`并让intent永久spent；readiness failure、
close、root retirement或Host failure以后都不refund。Kernel先capture/validate完整prepared install，再运行CAS gate，随后只做state
assignment及no-throw composite finalize，最后unlock并按existing transient-before-state order通知。因此listener reentry只能观察到
**spent intent + installed child + suspended parent** 的完整状态：repeat redemption为`stale`；listener在后续transition立即retire child
时，outer `preparing`仍只描述已提交的edge，opaque candidate可已经stale，但不得出现unspent-installed或spent-uninstalled中间态、
phantom preparation record或重复notification。

S4.2.1只冻结preparation。Future `.2`以preparation private record接入Host lease/Host-commit ready/fail；`.3`接入preparing fallback
dismiss、active `player.toggle_history`/`ui.cancel`、Back/Escape/backdrop/routed-cancel及opener focus ledger；`.4`才把History/blocker
transition与reveal/Auto/Skip timer remaining放进同一CAS gate。`.1` tests可以经stable-composite test seam settle/retire candidate以证明
structural retain/cascade，但不得发布Narrative readiness/fail/close API或把该test seam冒充Host delivery。

#### S4.2.1 mutation-sensitive RED and exact scope

S4.2.1必须先以以下RED失败，且失败只来自上述API/behavior尚未实现：

1. **Cross-axis parent proof**：stable Dialogue ready instance不在transient vector；ordinary reducer/generic Coordinator不能用raw/
   copied/foreign handle打开History。Same-kernel exact direct proof成功，foreign kernel/authority/owner/source/target、retained或
   suspended proof稳定zero；first exact composite claimant、same-claimant retained reuse、foreign/clone/borrowed claimant zero-read throw，
   same-kernel predecessor bridge dispose→successor bridge复用composition claimant但old lifecycle stale，cross-axis child的parent ID、
   structurally derived state lineage/runtime attempt与single slot exact。
2. **Result/opacity**：claim retained-singleton、borrowed method fail closed；commit guard exact one-method shape/descriptor capture与candidate
   argument，generic candidate frozen zero-key、`installed | stale | faulted` exact keys，Narrative preparation frozen zero-key及三行family
   result exact keys/completion、fresh/canonical identity；全部新name在UI root、`./internal`与package exports有type/runtime negative guard。
3. **Post-mint freshness**：admission dispose/recreate alone仍可prepare；parent replacement preparation、source/frame/readiness retry、
   temporary suspend→resume、empty、bridge/application/Coordinator terminal、clone/foreign/repeat全部stale且zero allocation/notification；
   two-intent loser unspent但child close后仍永久stale，fresh vacancy只接受fresh intent。
4. **Atomic gate/rollback**：plan/capacity/composite/reducer fault为unspent `faulted`并证明state identity、high-water、topology/publication、
   listeners及preparation record zero；forced prepared-token stale、CAS false/abort为unspent `stale`。Only installed preparation spends；
   repeat stale。Mutation must kill a version that spends before plan, installs before spend, refunds after install或在fault留phantom record。
5. **Listener reentry**：transient及state listener分别repeat same intent，均只见spent+installed并返回stale；listener immediate structural
   retirement只发生在next transition，outer仍返回fresh historical `preparing`且late preparation proof stale。Notification order/count与
   listener throw containment沿用kernel contract。
6. **Retain/cascade**：open commit一次性安装History `preparing / child_open` fallback并使exact stable root suspended；replacement
   preparation retain exact root+History，replacement failure identity-exact retain；ready cutover、greater-empty与bridge/publisher/
   Coordinator dispose一次性retire两者且不reparent。Duplicate exact-parent slot、orphan/dangling/foreign provenance fail closed。
7. **Boundedness**：10,000轮prepare→structural retire、replacement retain/fail/cutover、empty/dispose successor harness证明production
   strong state只保留per-kernel composition claimant/authority、current bridge lifecycle、current cross-axis link/preparation及scalar cursors；
   same-kernel bridge successor不累积claimant，不积累intent/candidate/parent tombstone、array、revision、subscriber history或second
   topology store，
   caller-retained opaque WeakMap keys不算production strong retention。

### S4.2.1 delivery — atomic History intent redemption + cross-axis child preparation（已完成）

本delivery严格落在已冻结的七文件scope。Reducer新增source-relative pure
`deriveManagedSurfaceReducerCrossAxisChildPreparationInternalV1()`、
`ManagedSurfaceReducerCrossAxisParentProjectionInternalV1`与
`DeriveManagedSurfaceReducerCrossAxisChildPreparationInputInternalV1`：它只接受composite已经认证的stable parent projection与fresh
candidate，要求parent不在transient publication、phase为active、owner/layer一致、child placement与exact resolved `single` slot正确、
shared identity cursor连续且该parent/slot未占用，再只生成一个`preparing / child_open` transient child。Ordinary reducer
`prepare_child`与generic Coordinator仍不能用raw/copy/foreign parent伪造cross-axis child。

Stable composite新增source-relative
`ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildAuthorityInternalV1`、
`claimManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildAuthorityInternalV1(kernel, exactClaimant)`、exact frozen one-method
`ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildCommitGuardInternalV1`、zero-key
`ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildCandidateInternalV1`与
`ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildPreparationResultInternalV1`。每个exact kernel的first composition claimant独占authority，same
claimant复用同一retained authority；foreign claimant/kernel与borrowed receiver在读取proof/definition/guard前以exact claim error
zero-read失败。Narrative family以per-kernel private claimant cache让same-kernel bridge successor复用composition authority，同时让每个
`NarrativeStableHistoryChildLifecycleInternalV1`继续绑定exact active bridge；old bridge/lifecycle/intent不会因successor或later vacancy复活。

Narrative新增`createNarrativeStableHistoryChildLifecycleInternalV1({ bridge })`与唯一
`redeemHistoryOpenIntentInternalV1(intent)`，成功只返回fresh frozen zero-key
`NarrativeStableHistoryChildPreparationInternalV1`及exact
`{ kind: "preparing", preparation, completion: null }`；`stale | faulted`仍为canonical bounded rows。Lifecycle从bridge-private record取得
exact kernel、composition claimant/authority、direct parent/source/frame与intent provenance，不接受caller-supplied kernel、raw parent/
instance/readiness或generic receipt/code。上述新name以及cross-axis reducer helper均只允许source-relative direct import；UI root、
`./internal` barrel与package exports的type/runtime negative guards保持green。

Redemption只在authentic unspent intent、same bridge/application/Coordinator、exact current ready-active non-retained parent proof、same
parent/source/frame且callback/semantic claims均空时进入pure plan。Plan先完成proof、owner/layer/slot/cardinality/occupancy、shared-cursor与
whole-composite lineage validation，再由existing `prepareStateInstallInternalV1(expectedState, nextState)`捕获完整successor。Capacity、
coherence、reducer/reflow或cross-axis invariant fault保持intent unspent，并让state identity、high-water、publication/topology、listener与
candidate/preparation record exact zero；known proof/currentness drift、prepared stale/invalid或guard false/abort返回unspent `stale`。

Narrative在preflight阶段预造preparation/result/guard。Prepared gate内先用descriptor-captured exact receiver/callable执行一次no-throw CAS：
复验intent仍未spent、bridge/authority/parent/source/frame及shared claims仍exact，只有winner写`spent = true`并接受同一opaque candidate。
Stable authority随后在同一true gate、state assignment前以预捕获no-throw WeakMap setter认证candidate的installed child/readiness/provenance；
false、throw或prepared stale路径不登记phantom candidate。Gate返回true后kernel只执行state assignment与no-throw composite finalize，故
**只有实际installed preparation会spend**，readiness failure、close、parent retirement或Host failure以后都不refund。两个intent竞争同一
vacancy时first winner install/spend；loser保持unspent但其topology-bound proof永久stale，child后来retire也不能复活，fresh vacancy必须
fresh mint intent。

Kernel继续按transient-before-state顺序同步通知。两类listener reentry都只观察spent intent、installed child与suspended exact parent，
repeat redemption返回canonical stale；listener若立即在下一transition structural-retire child，outer仍返回fresh historical
`preparing`，late preparation provenance只会成为stale，不出现unspent-installed、spent-uninstalled、重复notification或post-notify
candidate registration。Subscriber throw containment沿用既有runtime kernel合同。

Whole-composite validation不新增第二cross-axis topology collection，而从authorized state lineage、stable runtime attempt、shared-cursor
child instance/`parentInstanceId`、exact owner/layer/slot与single cardinality逐次结构化推导edge。History blocking preparation与future
ready test seam都使exact Dialogue root suspended；root replacement preparation保留exact parent + History，replacement failure继续
identity-exact保留。Ready cutover、greater-empty、publisher/bridge disposal与Coordinator terminal在同一install中cascade retire；
preparing child使用pure `readiness_failed` reducer oracle，ready child使用current-evidence `close_expected`，greater-empty在prospective
stable topology preliminary projection前先完成同一pure cascade，避免dangling child被提升成root或阻断合法empty transition。Orphan、
duplicate slot、foreign owner/layer、invalid cursor或dangling parent全部fail closed，且不会reparent到successor。

Stable-composite与Narrative各以10,000轮prepare→structural retire及replacement/empty/dispose successor覆盖boundedness：production strong
state只保留per-kernel current claimant/authority、active bridge lifecycle、current cross-axis preparation/link与scalar cursor；不累积intent、
candidate、parent tombstone、revision/subscriber history、array或第二topology store。Caller仍持有opaque WeakMap key所保留的historical
record不计入production strong retention，same-kernel bridge successor也不累积claimant。

preparation failure delivery API，没有实现S4.2.3
close/dismiss/input/focus、S4.2.4 timer/player suspension、S4.2.5.1c Engine Lab rig或S4.3 live cutover；generic Coordinator/contracts/action

digest/replay/wire与live features/development文档均保持zero diff；architecture只同步本delivery的已实现package/runtime边界。

public/generic receipt、code、export、publisher、Host或lifecycle store。

`.1` exact source-relative names固定为`NarrativeStableRootPreparationInternalV1`、
`NarrativeStableReadinessEntryInternalV1`、`NarrativeStableReadinessSnapshotInternalV1`、
`NarrativeStableHostLeaseInternalV1`、`NarrativeStableSessionInternalV1`及
`createNarrativeStableSessionInternalV1({ bridge })`。Root preparation是frozen zero-own-key token；History entry必须复用S4.2.1
`NarrativeStableHistoryChildPreparationInternalV1`，不能按definition/slot/instance ID重建。Entry closed union只有
`{ kind: "root", preparation: rootPreparation } | { kind: "history", preparation: historyPreparation }`；snapshot exact shape只有
frozen `{ entries }`，root-first且最多两项。Session强持有one current snapshot；entry vector未变的重复read必须返回same exact snapshot
identity，只有vector exact变化才mint fresh frozen vector/snapshot，满足`useSyncExternalStore` identity语义。

`NarrativeStableSessionInternalV1` exact methods为`getReadinessSnapshotInternalV1()`、
`subscribeInternalV1(listener)`、`getHistoryChildLifecycleInternalV1()`与
`attachHostInternalV1({ hostIdentity })`；lease exact methods只有`isCurrentInternalV1()`与`releaseInternalV1()`。`session.ts`只拥有上述
exported source-relative type declarations；factory、runtime records、bridge/lifecycle WeakMap访问与全部mutation留在
`narrative-managed-surface-family.ts`，不新增raw bridge accessor、binding claim或未冻结helper export。

每个exact active bridge缓存one session与one History lifecycle；重复factory返回same identity，lifecycle先建时session必须adopt exact
cached lifecycle。Same-kernel bridge successor继续复用S4.2.1 per-kernel claimant/one-method preparation authority，但session/lifecycle均fresh，
disposed predecessor的session/lease/lifecycle/intent/preparation永久stale。History redemption必须把prebuilt preparation private record及
bridge-private eventual-session current slot登记移入existing prepared-install commit guard：即使session尚未创建也先登记，later session
adopt same exact slot；generic candidate已认证、intent CAS winner确定之后、composite
assignment与同步notification之前，以pre-captured no-throw WeakMap setter/identity assignment完成。Guard false/nonboolean/throw、prepared
stale/abort或competitor loss均zero registration；installed listener第一次read session时必须已见same exact History entry。Root token从current
stable preparing binding按attempt identity惰性memoize，retire后不留strong tombstone。

Session factory的extra/missing/accessor/foreign/terminal bridge或borrowed receiver统一抛
`ui.narrative_stable_session_invalid`且zero claim/read；session method wrong receiver同码。Bridge terminal后snapshot read保持total并返回same
cached empty identity，subscribe返回idempotent no-op unsubscribe，History lifecycle read返回same stale lifecycle；只有attach按下述invalid抛错。

Host attach只以descriptor capture接受exact own-data `{ hostIdentity }`。Invalid/stale session在任何read前抛
`ui.narrative_stable_host_attachment_invalid`，distinct/parallel Host在任何kernel/snapshot mutation前抛
`ui.narrative_stable_host_lease_conflict`。Same identity reattach产生fresh generation并立即fence predecessor；release先idempotently关闭own
generation，microtask只在logical lease仍指向该released generation且无successor时清slot，不fail/close candidate、不改Surface/input/
notification。Session无dispose method；bridge terminal先fence session/lease/lifecycle、退订并把current snapshot推进为cached empty，再走既有
structural disposal，late callback只能stale或抛invalid。

`.1` mutation-sensitive RED至少覆盖：

1. factory/session/lease/root token/entry/snapshot的exact own keys、frozen/zero-key shape及root+`./internal`+package negative guards；
2. same bridge identity reuse、lifecycle-before-session adoption、same-kernel bridge successor fresh session/lifecycle且claimant identity不漂移；
3. `[] | [root] | [history] | [root, history]`、root-first/max-two、unchanged snapshot exact identity、vector变化fresh identity，以及ready/
   failed/retired/foreign/ABA exclusion；
4. History guard installed-only pre-notify registration，false/throw/stale/competitor zero，listener/reentrant subscriber第一次read即完整；
5. same Host generation rotation、release/reattach microtask、distinct Host zero-read conflict、bridge terminal与late callback；
6. malformed/accessor/extra-key/borrowed receiver、throwing listener/unsubscribe containment与listener order；
7. `10k` snapshot/attach/release/retry/successor churn只保留current root token、History preparation、Host lease、snapshot与subscriber set，
   无generation/preparation history、timer/tombstone/second topology store；
8. ready/fail、action/input/focus、renderer/portal/History content、close/dismiss、timer与live graph exact zero delta。

`commitInternalV1(contract: ManagedSurfacePreparedInputBindingContractInternalV1 | null): boolean`。Root新增kernel source-relative

cross-axis History也必须保持zero delta；下述`.2.2.2.0`已把current protected child的applied-only fence冻结为

Two-phase binding names固定为zero-key `ManagedSurfacePreparedInputBindingContractInternalV1`、

router/current contract，不推进publication、不替换predecessor；commit one-shot/no-throw，仅plain-read module-owned identity/generation/bool并做

每个exact active bridge只缓存one retained session与one retained History lifecycle；repeated factory返回same identity，

source-relative lazy terminal check覆盖factory、subscribe、attach、lease current及snapshot read。较早注册的raw kernel listener不能在
terminal state assignment后、session listener前mint lease或重建session；captured subscriber vector中listener A若同步terminalize bridge，

History，形成frozen `[] | [root] | [history] | [root, history]`、root-first、max-two vector。Entry exact identity/order未变时repeated read
复用same snapshot identity；只有vector变化才mint fresh frozen snapshot/vector并通知subscriber。Ready、failed、retired、foreign与ABA
preparation均不可见，unrelated stable reflow不通知；listener throw隔离、unsubscribe idempotent，terminal之后无subscriber delivery。

History preparation/private record/eventual-session current slot已移入S4.2.1 installed path的prepared-install commit guard：generic candidate
已认证且intent CAS胜出后、composite assignment与同步notification前，以captured native WeakMap setter与plain identity assignment完成登记。

`attachHostInternalV1`只descriptor-capture exact own-data `{ hostIdentity }`。一个session只保留one logical Host identity；distinct/parallel
identity在kernel/snapshot mutation前冲突，same identity reattach领取fresh generation并立即fence predecessor。Release idempotently关闭own
generation，per-session最多只有one scheduled microtask；same-host successor在grace内阻止旧task ABA-clear，released grace内distinct Host仍冲突，
无successor时才清logical slot。Attach/release不settle/fail/close root或History，不改Surface topology、notification、action/input/focus。

scalar/bool slot；没有generation/preparation history、timer、tombstone、append-only array或second topology store。

`.2.2.2.1`的prepared action contract新增source-relative
`PrepareManagedSurfaceContractBoundActionBindingInputInternalV1`，exact own-data fields只有
`authority`、`inputContextId`、`inputRouter`、`isGestureCurrent`及optional `registerManagedInputHandler`。Factory exact为
`prepareManagedSurfaceContractBoundActionBindingInternalV1(input): ManagedSurfacePreparedContractBoundActionBindingInternalV1`；prepared
handle exact frozen methods只有
`commitInternalV1(contract: ManagedSurfacePreparedInputBindingContractInternalV1): boolean`、`abortInternalV1(): void`与
`getBindingInternalV1(): ManagedSurfaceActionBindingV1 | null`。Prepare descriptor-capture input并可建立inert router dispatcher，但不分配
future full contract、不替换current binding、不推进input publication、不开放consumer；generic pure readiness successor才给ready guard提供
exact future full contract。

Raw full contract不得在prepared install gate内结构化读取；`.1`另增frozen zero-own-key
`ManagedSurfacePreparedInputBindingContractInternalV1`及
`captureManagedSurfacePreparedInputBindingContractInternalV1(contract: ManagedSurfaceInputBindingContractV1):
ManagedSurfacePreparedInputBindingContractInternalV1`。Generic pure plan在进入transition前完成raw contract parse/canonical validation并capture
WeakMap-auth token；token private record绑定exact frozen full contract与origin，clone/foreign/stale token不能从own keys重建。
`commitInternalV1(contractToken)` one-shot/no-throw地只认证token并CAS same router、reserved prepared slot与expected current，成功后
`getBindingInternalV1()`才返回exact binding；commit前、abort后、false/stale路径均返回`null`。Abort idempotently撤销prepared slot；same handle
repeat commit稳定false。

Prepared consumer必须在commit前以
`claimManagedSurfacePreparedAuthenticatedActionRouteInternalV1<TAttempt, TResult>(prepared, consume):
ManagedSurfaceAuthenticatedActionRouteInternalV1<TAttempt, TResult>`预装；first exact claim独占，borrowed receiver、clone、foreign/aborted
prepared或second claim在调用consumer/router前以existing action-route claim invalid fail closed。Root replacement与preparing History可以同时
存在，因此每个exact `(InputRouterV1, inputContextId)` production state精确有one stable managed dispatcher、one current record与
**最多two inert prepared records**（每个exact authority只保留latest）；prepared record不另注册handler，也不保留retired cleanup/
unregister history。First dispatcher creation descriptor-capture并冻结registrar identity；later prepare提供alternate registrar时不得读取、调用或
替换它：existing `(router, context)`路径只验证required own-data fields并忽略optional registrar descriptor/value，不把alternate registrar误报成
identity conflict。Third distinct authority必须在registrar/router read及revision reserve前以
`ui.managed_surface_input_authority_conflict` fail closed。Same-authority newer prepare必须在transition fence外terminal-abort并替换旧prepared，
不得把同一authority累积成第二slot。任一prepared first commit pointer-wins，另一handle因expected-current token漂移稳定false；
只有其Surface candidate仍current preparing时才可基于new current mint fresh prepared token重试，已cascade/retired candidate不得复活。
Replacement commit只做module-owned pointer/revision swap，不能在
kernel transition fence内调用registrar/unregister、router、consumer或gesture callback。Prepare只从private scalar high-water reserve fresh
input publication revision，不改变current publication；abort/stale永久burn gap且不rollback/reuse，commit才发布reserved revision。Old logical
binding立即stale；inactive dispatcher稳定ignored，同步listener reentry必须已经路由到new logical binding。

Shared `ManagedSurfaceStableReadinessCommitGuardInternalV1`仍为frozen exact one-method own-data object，但其唯一method精确修正为
`commitInternalV1(contract: ManagedSurfacePreparedInputBindingContractInternalV1 | null): boolean`。Root ready继续使用
`settleStableReadinessReadyWithCommitGuardInternalV1(envelope, commitGuard)`，Host-owned root failure新增source-relative
`settleStableReadinessFailedWithCommitGuardInternalV1(envelope, commitGuard)`；existing unguarded failed method不作为Host路径。
History继续使用独立same-claimant
`ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildReadinessAuthorityInternalV1`、
`claimManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildReadinessAuthorityInternalV1(kernel, exactClaimant)`与
`ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildReadinessResultInternalV1 =
Readonly<{ kind: "applied" }> | Readonly<{ kind: "stale" }> | Readonly<{ kind: "faulted" }>`。Authority exact methods为
`settleExactParentTransientChildReadinessReadyInternalV1(candidate, commitGuard):
ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildReadinessResultInternalV1`与
`settleExactParentTransientChildReadinessFailedInternalV1(candidate, commitGuard):
ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildReadinessResultInternalV1`；不得给
S4.2.1 one-method preparation authority追加key。Same preparation claimant重复claim返回same retained readiness authority，missing/different
claimant、foreign kernel或borrowed receiver仍在candidate/state read前抛
`ui.managed_surface_stable_exact_parent_transient_child_readiness_claim_invalid`。

Cross-axis ready child action另增closed
`ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildActionInputCaptureResultInternalV1 =
Readonly<{ kind: "captured"; contract: ManagedSurfaceInputBindingContractV1 }> |
Readonly<{ kind: "unavailable" }> |
Readonly<{ kind: "faulted"; code: "surface.stable_reconcile_faulted" }>`及
`ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildActionRouteAuthorityInternalV1`。该authority extends
`ManagedSurfaceContractBoundActionRouteAuthorityInternalV1`，新增唯一capture member
`captureCurrentExactParentTransientChildInputInternalV1(candidate: unknown):
ManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildActionInputCaptureResultInternalV1`；claim exact为
`claimManagedSurfaceStableExactParentTransientChildActionRouteAuthorityInternalV1(kernel, exactClaimant)`。它必须命中S4.2.1 exact kernel的
same composition claimant，same claimant复用，foreign/missing claimant、kernel、candidate或borrowed receiver在读contract/state前抛
`ui.managed_surface_stable_exact_parent_transient_child_action_claim_invalid`。Only authenticated current ready History可captured；preparing、
failed、retired、parent/source/slot drift与ABA successor均unavailable。它只验证并返回`surface.action_routed` continuation gate，不取得close/
dismiss authority。

Root/History ready或failed的atomic protocol固定为：先pure-plan exact whole-composite successor并从该successor派生future full input contract，
在transition外capture exact contract token或选择exact `null`，再取得prepared-state install token；ready/failure gate收到该exact token后，先调用Host commit的no-throw
prepared-binding CAS/abort，再只做module-owned
candidate phase、Host generation与focus-registration ownership pointer swap，随后返回true；kernel才assignment并同步notify。Gate不得调用
除该exact captured guard自身以外的router/registrar/unregister、DOM、React、Story、History observation、bridge、Coordinator、semantic
consumer或任意caller callback。
Malformed/accessor/extra-key guard、throw或non-boolean为generic `faulted` zero；stale epoch/candidate/source/lease/prepared token、guard false、
binding contract drift或reentry winner为generic `stale` zero。Only installed generic `applied`映射Narrative `settled`；listener首见ready时
Surface、binding、candidate provenance、parent suspension/resume与Host-local focus registration必须已经同批一致。Listener同步replace/dispose/
release后outer仍返回historical applied/settled，repeat与opposite receipt永久stale。

Failure与ready必须共用同一guard type。Root initial failure的pure successor提供`null`，guard在assignment前abort candidate prepared binding并
保持无Narrative current binding；root replacement failure与History failure的pure successor提供恢复后exact retained-root full contract，Host
必须在transition外capture token、预备retained-parent successor binding并在同一gate提交它、同时abort candidate binding。History claimed failed exact method因此修正为
`settleExactParentTransientChildReadinessFailedInternalV1(candidate, commitGuard)`；不得保留无guard Host failure旁路。同步listener首见failed/
resumed state时已经看见恢复后的root binding contract，旧topology contract永久stale；focus registration仍指向exact retained root。
Ready/fail first winner独占terminal state：ready accepted后late failure不能倒写，failed accepted后late ready/ready commit不能复活；planning/internal
fault不得assignment、notification、binding commit或消费仍可重试的exact preparation。

Generic bypass在stable-composite finalizer按**stale evidence first、applied-only fence second**冻结。Stale application epoch、instance/
readiness evidence、routing lease或owner proof保持既有exact stale/rejected code；只有ordinary generic transition本来会applied到authenticated current
cross-axis History时才回滚到exact current composite state，并统一返回`rejected / surface.invalid_transition`。Fence覆盖ordinary readiness ready/
failed、`close_expected`/`closeTop`、dismiss、`ui.cancel`/`player.toggle_history` route、selective owner cancel/dispose与任何直接child mutation；
topology revision、input publication、cursor、binding与notification均zero。Claimed History ready/fail、S4.2.1 structural retention及ready cutover/
greater-empty/publisher bridge disposal/Coordinator terminal的whole-composite cascade必须继续applied，不能被generic fence误拦。`.3`未来只能新增
exact current History close/dismiss authority，不能删除该generic fence。

`.2.2.2.1` mutation-sensitive RED至少覆盖：

1. prepared input/contract token/handle/claim、shared guard、History readiness/action authority/capture/result的exact keys、frozen rows、descriptor/accessor/
   extra-key/borrowed receiver及UI root、`./internal`、package export type/runtime negative guards；
2. prepare保持predecessor current，full contract只由pure successor传入；commit true/false、abort/repeat、expected-current drift、second
   prepared competitor及preclaim dispose，且false/stale/fault无router/publication delta；root replacement + History同时占用two prepared slots，
   first commit使另一旧token stale，candidate仍current才可fresh retry，third distinct authority pre-register conflict；prepare reserve、abort/stale
   burn gap、commit publish及revision ABA不复用；
3. ready guard先binding/focus CAS、后Surface assignment、再同步notification；listener即时route/release/replacement/dispose与subscriber throw containment
   不产生ready-then-effect、double route或post-notify registration；
4. root与History各自ready/fail first-win、guard false/throw/nonboolean、plan fault、prepared stale、foreign candidate/claimant/kernel与same-kernel
   claimant reuse；raw contract在gate前capture，clone/foreign token zero；initial root failure传`null`，replacement/History failure传
   restored-root token，listener首见failure即命中新contract；
5. generic readiness、closeExpected/closeTop、dismiss、two History action IDs、owner cancel/dispose的current protected-child
   `rejected / surface.invalid_transition`，以及stale evidence precedence、claimed settlement与structural cascade negative controls；
6. retained root + History + replacement preparation/failure保持exact old binding，ready cutover只切一次new binding并同install cascade old aggregate；
7. 10,000轮prepare/abort/commit/stale/replacement对每个exact router/context只保留one stable dispatcher、one current、最多two
   latest-per-authority prepared、scalar revision high-water与current claimant authorities，无per-binding handler、retired cleanup、unregister/
   revision/candidate/tombstone/append-only array history；later alternate registrar zero-read。

上述每个新增generic top-level type/function/claim/capture name都必须在UI root与`./internal`各有`@ts-expect-error` type import negative及runtime
inventory negative；prepared handle、guard、root guarded ready/failed、History authority ready/failed与child action capture的每个member spelling都
必须加入exact member inventory。不得向public coordinator/result/code、UI barrel或package export新增同名或别名入口。

`.2.2.2.1` implementation scope严格只有以下**5 files**：

- `engine/packages/ui/src/managed-surfaces/managed-surface-action-route.ts`及同名`.test.ts`；
- `engine/packages/ui/src/managed-surfaces/managed-surface-stable-composite-state.ts`及同名`.test.ts`；
- `engine/packages/ui/src/public-api.test.ts`。

Broader UI matrix若命中本entry刻意替换的旧per-binding unregister或ordinary generic History-readiness旁路，额外只允许更新以下
**5个test-only characterization files**：

- `engine/packages/ui/src/managed-surfaces/managed-surface-coordinator-lifetime.test.ts`；
- `engine/packages/ui/src/managed-surfaces/managed-surface-composition-runtime.test.ts`；
